# SMH Strategy v0: the Salient Market Hypothesis as a concrete paper-trading strategy

Status: CONSOLIDATION DOCUMENT (12 Nov 2026, session 56, PROGRESS block 64), answering Andrea's directive: continue developing more concretely the ideas behind SMH as a paper-trading profitable strategy, starting again from the principles drawn, explaining how each idea was tested and developed sequentially into robust data. Documentation only: every number below is a frozen, selftest-asserted design constant already recorded in the cited bundle documents; no new computation was performed, nothing was logged, nothing was booked. DESIGN BOOKKEEPING on the UNSEALED pre-2021 sample (rule-set-V class) wherever a number appears; the holdout 2021+ is SPENT and never touched; the forward log is empty by design and is the only path to trust. Nothing here changes T, V, V4h, the kill lines, the probabilities, the register or the forward protocol.

## 1. What the strategy is, in one paragraph

The SMH is not a signal that trades. It is an actuarial table for the market's significant weeks, run live as a paper system with two parallel bookkeeping streams. Stream one books WHAT THE MACHINE KNOWS: every time the clock registers an entry (a cross into compression, a cross into stress), a forecast row is logged in advance with the frozen hazards (for example 0.634 for a significant week within 12 weeks of a compression entry), scored when its window resolves, and judged against the do-nothing climatology by pre-declared kill lines and reliability readouts. Stream two books WHAT THE MACHINE DOES: three weekly paper tracks (T, U3, V) and one 4h paper bot (V4h), frozen rules that implement the measured laws (trend persistence for direction, vol targeting for exposure), booked every week including no-trade weeks, audited by an independent read-only tool. The two streams meet in one falsification schedule: the calibration record is the product being built, and it is the only thing the central theorem permits to be privately valuable. Nothing in this document claims the strategy is profitable yet; the entire content of "profitable" is decomposed in section 6 into questions the schedule can kill or confirm in public.

## 2. The principles it is built on

The six unconditional laws (canvas section 3), each with its operational use:

1. Volatility persists, then mean-reverts. USE: the clock itself (sig_w terciles, the compression and stress edges).
2. Tails are heavy. USE: all thresholds, stops and event definitions live in sigma units, never fixed points (the significant week is 2.5 x sig_w of its own decision week).
3. Autocorrelation has horizon structure. USE: the directional prior, valid only inside trend states at the right horizons - this is rule set T's licence.
4. Impact is concave in volume. USE: a significant event must be confirmed by volume; a move without volume is suspect (measured at the weekly grain in the anatomy layer).
5. Events self-excite. USE: hazard is state-dependent, so timing forecasts are conditional distributions, never constants.
6. Every exploitable regularity decays. USE: every rule carries a forward calibration score and a decay watch; kill lines and readouts exist because of this law.

The central theorem (canvas section 4): in a public market, a rule that identified an event as certain, timed AND directional before it happens would be traded until it stopped working; such a rule cannot exist publicly. So the claims are distributed: certainty lives at the level of the distribution (the laws), probability at the level of the event (conditional rates), hazard at the level of timing (the clocks), and the date and the sign are refused until forward rows earn them. The observer corollary: the only forecast that cannot be priced away is private, and it exists in exactly three forms - information others lack, speed others lack, or a calibration record others have not measured. The SMH builds the third. This is the entire honest content of "profitable" that the framework can aim at.

Andrea's five-layer architecture (docs/smh_architecture_v0.md), mapped to its measured state:

- Layer 1, market state: BUILT (the tercile clock, occupancy 27.5/30.6/41.9 percent, the dwell and transition machine).
- Layer 2, event probability and hazard: BUILT (both KM edge clocks, climatology, anatomy, aftermath, cycle, the full falsification machinery).
- Layer 3, AVFI and precursor signatures: FULLY ANSWERED, mostly null (transitions orthogonal; Cox covariates worse than duration out of fold, corrected session 54; sp_z closed null by coverage and decision test, session 54; three seductive cells registered-flagged-untrusted, register closed).
- Layer 4, direction and microstructure: half deliberately forward-only (two pre-declared direction peeks), half data-blocked (broker export for volume/Hawkes; options term structure for event-structure unseen-ness).
- Layer 5, entry, invalidation and exposure: exposure closed by verdict (session 52: armed-reduction dead, vol targeting the measured lever, descriptively); invalidation measured as the clock's own structures; costs measured immaterial at this grain; entry = the live paper tracks while the SMH books forecasts, not trades.

## 3. The sequential development ladder: how each idea was tested, and what it earned

Built in order, each rung answering the previous rung's open question. Evidence classes used throughout: MEASURED (frozen, selftest-asserted), SEDUCTIVE (measured but selected-looking, flagged, never trusted until forward-scored), FORWARD-ONLY (deliberately not tested in-sample), DATA-BLOCKED (cannot be computed with current data), REFUSED (the central theorem forbids the claim).

Rung 0, the discipline itself. The programme's prior results define what "robust" means here: 27 indicator variants dead in the H-number era; the AVFI state system scored 0.42 against random-entry 0.465 on the same window (the in-sample direction path, priced and refused); the bear-capitulation "edge" was one episode; the |sp_z| entry filter produced zero trades ever; the holdout battery for T failed at the margin (Sharpe 0.575, p 0.069 vs 0.05) and U3's too (0.418, p 0.116). Lesson frozen: an unsealed design sample proves nothing, a spent holdout proves nothing twice, and the only remaining evaluation path is pre-declared forward bookkeeping. Everything below is run under that constitution.

Rung 1, the state (Layer 1). Question: does volatility carry measurable structure? Test: 8-week rolling sig_w, tercile cuts frozen at the pre-2021 27.52nd/58.10th percentiles (LOW 0.01739, HIGH 0.02697). Result: the first measured tick - a 47 versus 14 percent significant-week spread across terciles. Evidence: MEASURED (the clock study, corrected and frozen). This is law 1 made operational.

Rung 2, the compression hazard clock (Layer 2). Question: after a cross into compression, when does the significant week come? Test: discrete Kaplan-Meier of the first significant week over the 41 pre-2021 compression entries, person-week construction, 327 rows, 26 first events, with a standing consistency check (sum of at-risk = person-weeks) adopted after an off-by-one in the first frozen table was caught by independent reconstruction. Result: cum 0.634 by week 12 [0.487, 0.782], median lag 5, front-loaded hazard peaking 0.148 at week 6; weekly event rate in compression 0.0969 versus 0.0448 unconditional (ratio 2.16). Evidence: MEASURED.

Rung 3, the salience control. Question: is the entry condition better than starting the same clock from any week? Test: the identical 12-week KM started from every decision week (the climatology, 1050 overlapping episodes). Result: 0.441 [0.411, 0.471]; the bands do not overlap (0.487 > 0.471); uplift 1.44x on the 12-week clock, 0.268 vs 0.168 at 4 weeks. THE SALIENCE IS EDGE-LOCAL: mid entries give 0.470 [0.349, 0.590], indistinguishable from climatology, and the gradient 0.634 > 0.470 > 0.441 > 0.273 is monotone. Evidence: MEASURED, with the frozen caveat that the overlapping-episode climatology band is too narrow in truth, a cut that favours the claim and is flagged for that reason.

Rung 4, the mirror clock. Question: does the other edge of the cycle carry the same structure? Test: the same KM on stress entries (n = 33), event = de-stress within 12 weeks. Result: 0.636 [0.472, 0.801], median lag 6 - the quarter rule, both edges resolving at two-thirds within a quarter; stress decays by calm, not crash (20 of 21 de-stresses without a significant week first); the stress side's own event clock is suppressed (0.273 [0.121, 0.425]). Evidence: MEASURED.

Rung 5, the state machine. Question: how do the regions and the AVFI states relate? Test: dwell tables, transition counts, joint occupancy. Result: stress dwells twice as long as compression (mean 13.3 vs 7.0 weeks); mid is the fast exchange (median 3); compression exits to mid 93 percent, jumps straight to stress only 3 of 41; the alternation Elliott named is the TRANSITION structure (momentum_up to reversal_down 13 of 13, back 11 of 13), while state and clock are nearly ORTHOGONAL in joint occupancy - the state cycles while the clock ticks, neither explaining the other. Evidence: MEASURED. This closed most of Layer 3 before Layer 3 was formally mapped.

Rung 6, the event's anatomy (laws 4 and 5 measured). Question: what IS the anticipated event? Test: dissection of the 26 first events. Result: the median event is a ~3.5-sigma week (overshooting its own 2.5-sigma bar 1.41x); significant weeks come on median volume 1.138x their trailing baseline (all weeks 1.004), 70 versus 51 percent above baseline, ZERO thin-flagged event weeks - effort confirms result, measured; the volume asymmetry (up events 0.938, down events 1.733, n = 17/9) is SEDUCTIVE, flagged, untrusted. The entry-anchored versus spell-anchored tension resolved: of 26 first events, 7 arrive strictly in-spell, 11 ARE the escape week, 8 arrive after escape - an act dissolved at spell end captures 0.439, not 0.634. Evidence: MEASURED (with the anatomy asymmetry flagged).

Rung 7, the aftermath and the circle. Question: what follows the print, and does the machine run on a line or a circle? Test: post-event KM, return-lane counts, actuarial rates. Result: the first print is usually the only print (22 of 26 windows without a further significant week; median gap between significant weeks anywhere 18.5); the print's durable product is the LEVEL (sig_w at the event 1.324x the entry week's - the threshold-chase mechanism, definition-bound and flagged); post-print suppression is real and disjoint-band (cum 0.231 by 12 weeks [0.069, 0.393] vs climatology 0.441); the circle closes in medians: print at 5 weeks, stress region 3 weeks later, de-stress on its own clock, next compression cross at a median 15 weeks after the print; the lanes split decay-first 14 of 25 (median 11.5 weeks) versus stress-first 11 (median 20) - the boring lane is the faster lane. Actuarial rates: 2.04 triggers and 1.29 entry-conditioned events per year, occupancy 27.5/30.6/41.9 percent, the three dwell tables tiling the sample exactly (1049.7 of 1050). Evidence: MEASURED.

Rung 8, the covariate program (Layer 3 completed, null). Question: does anything at entry - momentum sign, AVFI state, compression depth, the VXN level, the spread z-score - improve event TIMING beyond duration? Tests: the person-week cloglog hazard model with leave-one-episode-out Brier, corrected in session 54 after the pre-registered anchor validation caught the session-32 pair frozen from scratch prose (the corrected values: intercept-only 0.07364, duration-only 0.07406, full covariate model 0.07607 - the covariates are WORSE out of fold by 2.71 percent, and duration itself is indistinguishable from a constant weekly hazard); the priced-calm VXN split (the largest seductive cell the design produced: residual-fear half 0.800 [0.625, 0.975] versus deep-calm 0.476 [0.263, 0.690], era-confounded, registered-flagged-untrusted); the sp_z study (closed by coverage first - sp_z exists only from 2015-04-10, 10 of 41 entries carry it - and by the decision test second, 1.80 percent below the pre-declared 2.0 percent bar: ADDS NOTHING). Result: the hazard content of the compression signature is DURATION - honestly, the pooled level, not even the slope - not the entry conditions. Evidence: MEASURED, and the strongest null in the programme.

Rung 9, robustness and stability. Question: how much belongs to the phenomenon, and how much to the definition? And is the edge stable across decades? Tests: pre-committed definition-elasticity grids, one knob at a time, all cells reported (11 cells compression, 9 stress); 5-year-bucket trigger rates versus capture fractions. Result: the entry-minus-climatology GAP is positive in every compression cell (0.106-0.222 around the frozen 0.193) and the stress edge exceeds the coin in every stress cell (0.115-0.283 around 0.136); the frozen definition is NOT the grid optimum (anti-tuning evidence, a standing selftest anchor); the disjoint-band strength is definition-local (4 of 11, the frozen one included - the "bands do not overlap" sentence is never quoted without the frozen definition attached); the trigger rate swings 0.7 to 3.2 per year across buckets (4.5x) while the capture fraction holds (0.647/0.625 versus 0.634). ROWS ARE STABLE, YEARS ARE NOT: the measured justification for kill lines that count rows, never clocks. Evidence: MEASURED.

Rung 10, exposure (Layer 5's sizing question). Question: does clock-keyed exposure sizing lower max drawdown at comparable return? Test: the pre-registered six-cell grid (buy-hold, armed-half, vol-target, both, momentum, momentum-armed), no lookahead, all cells reported. Result: ALL THREE armed pairs FAIL, diagnostically - the armed halving changed max drawdown by exactly zero (every max-drawdown episode contains zero armed weeks) while cutting return 30-42 percent, and zero of the ten worst weeks were armed. THE MECHANISM, measured: the clock's event is SIGMA-RELATIVE, equity drawdown mass is SIGMA-LEVEL and lives in stress - the clock times scale-free events, the drawdown is made of scale. The lever that worked on this sample, descriptively, no decision attached: vol targeting (max drawdown -0.384 vs -0.695, total return HIGHER, Calmar nearly doubled) and the momentum rule (best Calmar of all six, 0.287). Evidence: MEASURED, verdict frozen (armed-reduction DEAD; the vol-target observation carried with no decision).

Rung 11, the falsification machinery. Question: what evidence would ever license the word "profitable", and what kills the machine in public? Test: pre-declared, before the first forward row exists. Result (all frozen): the compression occurrence kill lines 3/8/19/31 at 10/20/40/60 scored rows (each gate false-kills a true machine about 3-4 percent of the time; a climatology world dies at the gates with probability 0.286/0.447/0.724/0.905); the honesty statement frozen in advance - a TRUE machine dies somewhere in the schedule roughly one run in eight; the calibration expectations (if the design is right, SMH rows cost 0.6568 nats against climatology rows' 0.7319, an expected edge of 0.0751 nats per resolved window; symmetric if climatology is right); the reliability readouts at 52 and 104 scored windows; THE POSITIVE PROOF IS EXACTLY ONE STATEMENT: at least 57 of 104 compression windows resolve with the event (power 0.971, false-pass 0.018); direction gets exactly two pre-declared peeks (promotion at 27/40 then 39/60 all-up; down-bias annotation at 13/40 or 21/60), counted in resolved events; the seductive-cell register is CLOSED at three cells. Evidence: MEASURED (the schedule's own operating characteristics), all pre-declared before any forward data.

Rung 12, the live machinery. The forecast instrument (smh_forecast) drafts the rows at clock entries only and refuses otherwise; the resolver/scorer (smh_score) fills outcomes; the tracks (stage 7) book every week; the V4h bot books nightly with an independent read-only audit (session 55: the audit's own selftest bug - a pandas index-alignment false violation - was caught and fixed before first live use, and the live verdict stands CLEAN_NO_BOOKINGS); the whole chain selftests on every run: 24 checks. The forward log: EMPTY, by design, and it is the only proof object the programme owns.

That is the sequential development, and the honest summary of its robustness: eleven measured rungs, two registered-flagged seductive cells, one closed register, one dead sizing policy, one working (undeserved-by-decision) sizing lever, and a falsification schedule that was written by the machine against itself before it ever scored a live row.

## 4. The concrete live system: what runs, when, and what each part does

The paper portfolio, all NQ, all paper, all frozen:

- RULE SET T (the tracked rule): long at next Monday open when mom12 = log(close_t / close_{t-12}) > 0 at the Friday decision close, flat otherwise, signal exits only, cost 0.3625 pts/side, 100,000 USD paper equity. Law 3 in production. It failed the holdout battery at the margin (Sharpe 0.575, p 0.069); its only licence is that it is the programme's single replicated effect, and its only evaluation path is this forward track (26+ entries before any evaluation).
- RULE SET U3 (parallel, no claimed support): the vol-scaled T variant (state throttle 0.5, vol factor), logged for comparison because its battery also failed (0.418, p 0.116).
- RULE SET V (parallel): the from-scratch synthesis - T's signal plus vol-target sizing, size = min(1, 0.20/vol8) when long, 0.15 target in reversal_down, cap 1.0. Laws 3 and 1 in production; the sizing that rung 10 measured as the working lever, registered as a rule (not promoted by the study - the study was computed after V was registered; both facts are on the record).
- V4h (the 4h bot): the same law pair at the 4-hour grain, mom12h signal, vol8h targeting 0.20 (0.15 in reversal_down), cap 1.0, cost 0.3625 pts/side, multiplicative equity from 100,000, registered 2026-11-12T01:42:18Z, booking only completed bars via a nightly task, audited read-only by v4h_audit (currently CLEAN_NO_BOOKINGS: armed, not yet evaluated).
- THE SMH FORWARD LOG (the calibration stream): at every Sunday session, if the week registered a compression or stress entry, smh_forecast drafts the row (the frozen 4- and 12-week hazards, the direction split conditional on entry state) and appends it, guarded against duplicates and non-entry weeks - the protocol logs entries, not noise. smh_score resolves each row as its window completes and scores it by Brier and log loss against the climatology rows booked alongside.

The Sunday decision procedure (the operating cadence, verified in rehearsal session 50): smh_forecast status (clock state against the frozen cuts) -> theorem card -> stage0c canonical refresh -> stage7_outcome --append (resolve the tracks' pending week) -> stage7_log_entry --append (book the tracks' new decision) -> smh_forecast status/draft (book the SMH row if and only if an entry occurred) -> smh_score status -> the V4h audit verdict once bookings exist. Then verify_all. Current live state: decision week 2026-11-13 forming, sig_w 0.03723, STRESS region, stress spell age 5 weeks, reversal_down age 21 weeks, no compression or stress entry, no forecast row pending - Entry 0 (the tracks' start-up row, logged 2026-11-11, T flat at mom12 -10.69 percent) stands pending and resolves Sunday 2026-11-15.

What the SMH does NOT do live: it does not trade its own clock. The tracks' rules are frozen and unchanged; the SMH books forecasts about the market's structure while the tracks measure whether the laws-in-production survive costs and weekly decision friction. Connecting the two - for example, an SMH-conditioned entry overlay on the tracks - is a rule change, which requires a signed, dated amendment naming exactly what changed and why, and it is Andrea's to sign or refuse. What the measurements would say if that amendment is ever drafted: the entry act belongs AT the cross (waiting 4 quiet weeks forfeits 0.634 to 0.500, waiting 6 to 0.348 - timeliness is measured, not felt); the overlay must NOT be armed-window exposure reduction (dead by verdict); and any in-sample cell used to justify it (the 6/7 cell, reversal_down, residual fear) is flagged and earns nothing until forward-scored.

## 5. How the pieces connect (the strategy's logic in five sentences)

The laws say vol is a mean-reverting machine with heavy tails and horizon-structured autocorrelation (rungs 1, 5, 7). The clocks measure WHEN the machine's drama is actuarially loaded: the compression entry carries a 0.634 twelve-week significant-week hazard against 0.441 climatology, and the stress entry carries a 0.636 de-stress hazard against a coin (rungs 2-4). Nothing at entry improves the TIMING beyond the clock itself (rung 8), the edge survives every definitional perturbation as a GAP though not always as disjoint bands (rung 9), and the only exposure lever the data endorses is vol targeting, which needs no clock (rung 10). Therefore the only thing the SMH can honestly own is the calibration record - whether its logged hazards, resolved against reality, beat the do-nothing climatology in public, on a schedule written before the first row (rung 11). The paper tracks exist because laws 3 and 1 are the programme's only production-grade effects, and their forward equity - booked weekly under frozen conventions, audited, costed - is the separate, honest answer to whether the LAWS pay; the SMH's forward Brier and log scores are the answer to whether the CLOCK pays.

## 6. What "profitable" means here, decomposed into separately scored questions

1. CALIBRATION PROFIT (does the clock know something?). The SMH's logged rows are scored by Brier and log loss against climatology rows on the same resolved windows; reliability readouts at 52 and 104 windows; the kill lines 3/8/19/31 at 10/20/40/60 rows are the binding public test; the positive proof is at least 57 of 104 compression windows resolving with the event. If the design is right, the expected edge is 0.0751 nats per resolved window - which is a knowledge edge, and only a trading edge after Layer 5's arithmetic says so.
2. DIRECTION PROFIT (which way?). Refused in-sample, by the central theorem and by the priced history (the state system's 0.42 versus 0.465). Exactly two pre-declared forward peeks: promotion at 27/40 then 39/60 all-up first events; down-bias annotation at 13/40 or 21/60. No direction claim exists before those counts arrive at their own gates.
3. EXECUTION PROFIT (do the laws survive costs?). The tracks' paper equity, booked every week under the frozen conventions, 26+ entries before any evaluation, kill criteria pre-committed, the V4h bookkeeping audited row by row. The sizing study's arithmetic prices the honest expectations: the clock's event is sigma-relative, so clock-keyed exposure policies do NOT buy drawdown protection (dead by verdict); vol targeting and the momentum shape are the measured levers, carried descriptively.
4. UNSEEN-NESS (is any of this already priced?). The VXN benchmark stands registered as the test of true unseen-ness: the unseen-ness layer's finding is that the compression signature is LEVEL-CHEAP and MEAN-PRICED - the level costs little to observe, the hazard's timing content is not obviously in the price. Forward scoring of the SMH rows against what options markets charge is the standing data-blocked refinement (needs the options term structure).
5. THE TIMELINE, stated honestly because law 6 demands it. At the design trigger rate (2.04 triggers, 1.29 entry-conditioned events per year), the kill-line gates arrive in roughly 5/10/20/29 YEARS and the 104-window readout in roughly half a century - halved if both row windows count. Rows are stable, years are not (rung 9); the schedule counts rows, never years, and it is slow precisely because the machine triggers twice a year. Any acceleration of the trigger - a looser definition, a second market - would be a new pre-registered study, not an amendment to this one.

The one-sentence profit statement the programme can currently make: on unsealed design data the clock carries a real, definition-robust, covariate-clean timing asymmetry; whether that asymmetry is worth money after costs, in what direction, and for how long, are three separate questions the forward protocol scores separately - and it was built to be able to say NO in public.

## 8. What is refused, permanently

Dates for events; direction at a chosen time; covariate-improved timing claims; in-sample direction modelling; armed-window sizing overlays; any backtest on the spent holdout; any inferential language before 104 windows; any new scored cell without doc registration first; and any statement that the design sample's numbers are performance. The design sample is the actuarial table's construction data; the forward log is its life.

# SMH Stress Clock v0 (the mirror edge of the hazard layer)

Status: DESIGN BOOKKEEPING on the UNSEALED pre-2021 sample only (rule-set-V class). Date filter date < 2021-01-01 throughout; holdout 2021+ never touched. Built 12 Nov 2026, session 34. Same discipline as smh_hazard_v0.md: no inferential claims, no forward statistics, no trades; the forward protocol (logged rows, Brier/log scoring) is the only path to trust.

## 1. What this layer adds

The hazard layer measured one edge of the volatility cycle: compression entries and the significant week that follows them. This layer measures the other edge: STRESS entries (the cross ABOVE the frozen high-tercile cut) and the de-stress event (the next week in which sig_w falls back below the cut). Together the two edges close the clock: the SMH can now time both directions of the volatility cycle, expansion out of compression and calm out of stress, in the same survival-analysis form.

Definitions, frozen:

- Stress entry at t: sig_w(t) > HIGH (HIGH = 0.02697, the frozen pre-2021 upper tercile cutpoint) AND sig_w(t-1) <= HIGH. A cross, not a dwell. True crosses pre-2021: n = 33.
- De-stress event at t (event in week t+1): sig_w(t+1) <= HIGH. Threshold uses the frozen cut, not a rescaled one; the event is the exit from stress, not a price move.
- First de-stress: earliest h in 1..12 with the de-stress flag at decision week t0+h-1; person-week construction and the standing consistency check (sum of at-risk == person-weeks) per the session-32 policy. This table passes it: 258 = 258.

## 2. The frozen tables (pre-2021, unsealed)

Context rates: while in stress (440 high-tercile decision weeks) the 1-week de-stress rate is 0.0729; the 1-week significant-week rate while in stress is 0.0205, LESS THAN HALF the unconditional 0.0448. Partly mechanical (high sig_w raises the 2.5-sigma bar), and exactly the point: after a vol spike, a further vol-scaled significant week is rarer than the crowd's fear prices.

Kaplan-Meier discrete hazard of first de-stress after a stress entry (n = 33 entries):

| h (weeks since entry) | at risk | events | hazard | cum |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 33 | 3 | 0.091 | 0.091 |
| 2 | 30 | 4 | 0.133 | 0.212 |
| 3 | 26 | 1 | 0.038 | 0.242 |
| 4 | 25 | 1 | 0.040 | 0.273 |
| 5 | 24 | 0 | 0.000 | 0.273 |
| 6 | 24 | 2 | 0.083 | 0.333 |
| 7 | 22 | 2 | 0.091 | 0.394 |
| 8 | 20 | 4 | 0.200 | 0.515 |
| 9 | 16 | 2 | 0.125 | 0.576 |
| 10 | 14 | 2 | 0.143 | 0.636 |
| 11 | 12 | 0 | 0.000 | 0.636 |
| 12 | 12 | 0 | 0.000 | 0.636 |

Headline readings: 21 of 33 entries (0.636) de-stress within 12 weeks; median lag 6 weeks; 12 entries (36 percent) are STILL in stress after a full quarter (the persistent-stress tail). The KM cum at h = 4 is 0.273.

## 3. The three honest findings

1. THE QUARTER RULE (the layer's contribution to the theory): the two edges of the volatility cycle resolve at nearly the same speed. Compression to first significant week: cumulative 0.634 by week 12, median lag 5. Stress to de-stress: cumulative 0.636 by week 12, median lag 6. Both clocks give roughly a two-thirds probability that the regime edge resolves within a quarter, with a one-in-three tail that persists. Near-mirror symmetry across opposite edges is the kind of structure the framework predicts if the variance process is one mean-reverting machine clocking both directions. SUGGESTIVE at n = 41/33, not established.
2. STRESS DECAYS BY CALM, NOT CRASH: in 20 of the 21 de-stressed episodes, NO significant week occurred between entry and de-stress (the single exception was an up week). The de-stress week's own return is under 1 sigma in 0.644 of de-stress weeks. The crowd story after a vol spike is catharsis (the big flush that clears the air); the measured story is erosion (quiet weeks that roll the window's large returns out). CAVEAT, frozen: sig_w is an 8-week rolling window, so a large old return rolling out lowers sig_w without any new calm; the calm reading is partly mechanical and weekly data cannot fully separate erosion-by-calm from erosion-by-roll-off.
3. NOTHING CONDITIONS THE STRESS CLOCK: momentum at entry does not differentiate de-stress speed (medians 6 vs 7, means 5.4 vs 5.5), and no AVFI state does either (reversal_down median 6, momentum_up 6, momentum_down 8, all tiny n). Unlike the compression side's seductive direction cells, the stress side has NO seductive cell: the honest forecast is the unconditional table. This asymmetry is itself content: the compression edge carries (unproven) direction structure; the stress edge carries none in this sample.

## 4. Caveats, frozen

1. n = 33 entries; every cell is small-n; the double-bump hazard shape (h = 2 and h = 8-10) is noise-shaped and no shape claim is made.
2. Episode dependence: adjacent stress spells in the same high-vol regime are not independent.
3. The roll-off identifiability problem (section 3, point 2): the de-stress event mixes genuine calm with window mechanics; forward scoring against these frozen rows is the only separator.
4. All numbers are unsealed pre-2021 design bookkeeping. No claim, no test, no trade.

## 5. Forward protocol (mirror of the hazard layer's; same instrument)

research/smh_forecast.py (extended this session) drafts the stress forecast row when the latest decision week is a stress entry: de-stress probability within 4 weeks 0.273 and within 12 weeks 0.636, no direction split (none earned), the significant-week suppression noted as context. Same guards (Sunday cadence, fresh entry, append-only, no duplicates), same scoring (Brier/log as windows complete), same thresholds (reliability at 52/104 windows, no inferential claim before 104), same refusal to revise tables from post-2020 data.

# SMH theory consolidation draft v0 (the salient market hypothesis, assembled)

Status: THEORY-CLASS DOCUMENTATION under autonomy charter v1, class T,
item T-4. Written 19 Nov 2026, session 119, block 122, at the owner's
stated final goal (his 19 Nov 2026 message naming the salient market
hypothesis as the project's final goal). This is a CONSOLIDATION draft:
it assembles the already-frozen, already-measured pieces of the
programme into one coherent statement of the hypothesis. No new claim,
no new constant, no computation, nothing registered, nothing
evaluated, nothing booked. The word HYPOTHESIS is load-bearing: this
doc states a falsifiable structure with pre-declared proof and kill
machinery, never a held result. ZERO STATUS until the machinery it
describes accrues rows. The owner's novelty framing ("a new and first
of market theory") is his goal, not a fact this programme can
certify; section 9 states exactly what could and cannot ever make it
one. Every constant below was re-verified verbatim against its frozen
source this session (section 10).

## 1. The hypothesis in one paragraph

The salient market hypothesis (SMH) claims that a price series carries
recurrent, measurable, salience-local timing structure: a quiet-edge
entry condition - the 8-week realized volatility crossing DOWN into
its compression region - raises the conditional 12-week hazard of a
significant single-week move from the climatology 0.441 to a measured
0.634 with disjoint confidence bands; the content of the signature is
DURATION (a clock, anchored at the entry cross), not the entry
conditions themselves; the structure recurs actuarially (the next
cross follows the print at a median 15 weeks, the full circle in
medians ~29 weeks end to end); the public levels and means of the
phenomenon are PRICED (implied volatility tracks the clock
monotonically and the variance risk premium survives the trigger), so
the claim cannot rest on public-information mispricing; what remains
private, at the weekly grain, is the CALIBRATION RECORD - the act of
pre-registering the dated hazard and scoring it against realized
outcomes - and the entire hypothesis is falsifiable in public by
pre-declared kill lines (3/8/19/31 events at 10/20/40/60 resolved
rows) and a positive proof (exactly 57 of 104 resolved windows), with
direction refused until forward rows earn it. That is the whole
theory. Everything below is the frozen evidence for each clause, in
the programme's own order.

## 2. The refusal layer (why the hypothesis has this shape)

The SMH central theorem (session 30, block 38; consolidated sessions
36-37) is the certainty-timing-direction triangle: a public rule
claiming all three (certainty, timing, direction) is priced away, so
certainty lives in distributions, probability in events, hazard in
timing. Elliott Wave as received claimed all three and made zero
testable predictions, which is why it was refused as theory and kept
only as the descriptive canvas layer. The logic parents recorded at
the triangle's formalization: Bjork/Elliott-Kopp no-arbitrage, O'Hara
no-trade, McLean-Pontiff decay (publication decay as the empirical
anchor). The record's compressed statement of the refusal:
inevitability without timing and direction is true and priced; with
both it collapses back into statistics. There is no third door for a
PUBLIC rule; the refusal is a theorem, not a mood.

The observer corollary (session 32, block 40) is the constructive
turn: the only forecast that cannot be priced away is the private
one, existing in exactly three forms - information others lack, speed
others lack, a calibration record others have not measured. (The
queue's T-4 seed summary said "the two private advantages"; the
frozen wording is three forms, and this doc follows the frozen
wording.) SMH builds the third. Section 4 of the observer corollary
doc carries the privacy lemma P2 and its caveat: privacy erodes on
publication; the protective privacy of the record is the
PRE-REGISTRATION INTERVAL, and what remains strictly private even
after publication is the actuarial posture the record licenses -
sizing in proportion to the measured hazard. P2 is labelled
INTERPRETATION in the record; no computation in this programme
measures it.

The refusal layer is not decoration: it is what makes the rest a
hypothesis rather than a doctrine. Every time the programme faced a
claim that shortcut this triangle (the AVFI tops/bottoms belief in
Amendment 3; direction at design in the H-battery), it was tested as
directed and the null was frozen, or refused in advance by design.

## 3. The measured positive core (the five frozen facts)

All numbers are frozen, design-bookkeeping on unsealed pre-2021
weekly NQ data, and carry the record's standard caveat: design
statistics are bookkeeping, never proof; the proof object is the
forward calibration record alone.

3.1 TRIGGER (smh_hazard_v0.md, session 32): compression entry at t:
sig_w(t) <= LOW (LOW = 0.01739, the frozen pre-2021 tercile cutpoint)
AND sig_w(t-1) > LOW. A cross, not a dwell. True crosses pre-2021:
n = 41. sig_w is the 8-week trailing weekly log-return std (ddof=1)
including the current week.

3.2 EVENT (same doc): the significant 1-week event, |r1(t+1)| >= 2.5
x sig_w(t), first occurrence within 12 weeks of the entry. Anatomy
(session 39, smh_event_anatomy_v0.md): the realized first events
overshoot their own bar, median 1.41x (maximum 2.63x), so the median
significant week is a ~3.5-sigma week. The event is a significant
MOVE, never a signed one.

3.3 CLOCK (sessions 32-38; the Cox verdict in smh_strategy_v0.md
rung 8): entry-anchored Kaplan-Meier first-event clocks per region.
The hazard content of the compression signature is DURATION -
honestly, the pooled level, not even the slope - not the entry
conditions. Compression cum12 0.634 [0.487, 0.782], median lag 5
weeks, from 26 of 41 entries; matched climatology 0.441 [0.411,
0.471] from 1050 decision weeks. The corrected-table log (session
32: the off-by-one risk-set bug, 343 vs 327 person-weeks, caught by
the standing consistency check and corrected in the record) is part
of the frozen provenance.

3.4 SALIENCE GRADIENT (smh_mid_clock_v0.md, session 38, block 46):
the 12-week first-event cum by entry region - compression (n = 41)
0.634, mid (n = 66) 0.470, any week (n = 1050) 0.441, stress (n =
33) 0.273 - monotone, and THE SALIENCE IS EDGE-LOCAL: only the
compression cross separates from climatology with disjoint bands. In
the record's compressed form: above climatology at the cross, below
after the print (the post-print window is suppressed, cum 0.231 by
12 weeks [0.069, 0.393] vs climatology 0.441, disjoint-band; rung 7).

3.5 AFTERMATH AND THE QUARTER (smh_aftermath_v0.md, session 41; rung
7): the trigger recurs in ~a quarter - the next compression cross
follows the event at a median 15 weeks (one return censored at the
sample end), faster than the arrival of a fresh stress spell (median
23.5 weeks; every first event is followed by one in-sample, 26/26 -
a descriptive statement, not a probability claim). The full circle
in medians: cross -> print (5w) -> stress region (3w more, when it
comes within the quarter; 17 of 26) -> de-stress clock (6w from a
stress entry, cum 0.636 by 12w) -> next cross (15w from the print).
The modal episode is a single print against every yardstick (22 of
26 windows without a further significant week; the clustering
intensity is definition-made, median inter-arrival gap ~18.5 weeks,
with the endogenous-yardstick caveat frozen beside it; the lone-print
grid's exact session not re-derived, the record's honest 12 Nov 2026
sessions-57-71 currency window).

The capture ladder (sessions 37-38, the entry-theorem cards): enter
at the cross 0.634, after 4 quiet weeks 0.500, after 6, 0.348 -
timeliness is a measured margin, not a felt skill, and the 12-week
protocol window is the measured location of the asymmetry. Finer
timing than the cross needs intraday data the programme does not
hold.

## 4. The priced-ness layer (what the market already sees)

smh_unseen_v0.md, session 40, block 48 - the unseen-ness layer, the
market's own testimony on the signature:

- LEVEL-cheap: median VXN at compression entries 16.47 versus 20.14
  across all pre-2021 weeks; entries sit at the 25.8th percentile of
  VXN's own distribution (mid 46.3rd, stress 67.4th); by region
  occupancy, compression weeks at the 22.5th percentile (mid 42.4th,
  stress 77.7th). Implied volatility follows the clock, monotonically.
- MEAN-priced: the variance risk premium survives the trigger: +3.71
  vol points at entries (positive at 73.2% of entries) versus +4.55
  across all weeks (76.7%); forward 12-week realized at entries 13.96
  versus 17.22 all weeks. The strong unseen-ness claim ("the market
  fails to price the coming expansion") is REFUTED at the mean in the
  design sample: the crowd's calm is still overpriced calm, thinner
  premium, no inversion.
- Frozen consequence: unseen is the wrong word for cheap. The edge
  claim, if any, cannot rest on options mispricing at this grain; it
  rests on the event-structure sub-door (whether the specific DATED
  hazard priced by term structure differs from the clock - unmeasured,

  and on the forward calibration record.

Door status (the observer corollary's measured summary): door 1,

log is the proof object. So the hypothesis's private content is door

  no asymmetric tuning applied.

  in the four-gate schedule roughly one run in eight (~0.126,
  independence approximation, the gates share rows); and the design

  survivor on its own - the 1-in-8 honesty statement. Both statements
  prevent the programme from celebrating either survival or death
  without accounting for the schedule's own arithmetic.

  resolved compression 12-week windows resolve with the event (the
  stricter of the two rules). Power 0.971, false-pass 0.018.

  entry, design 16/20): examined only at n >= 10 forward rows,
  promoted only on a CP95 lower bound exceeding the unconditional
  forward event share, never automated, counted silently below the
  floor.
- Timing honesty: at the design trigger rate (2.04 triggers, 1.29
  entry-conditioned events per year), the occurrence gates arrive in
  roughly 5/10/20/29 YEARS and the 104-window readout in roughly half
  a century, halved if both row windows count. Rows are stable, years
  are not: capture fraction 0.647 vs 0.625 across decade halves while
  the trigger rate moves by a factor of 4.5 (regime range 0.7-3.2
  triggers/year). The schedule counts rows, never years. No dated
  instance is ever inevitable; inevitability is actuarial.

## 6. The known defects, stated inside the theory

A hypothesis that hides its own defects is a marketing document.
The record holds three:

6.1 The cell-promotion rule critique (smh_cell_promotion_critique_v0.md,
session 116, block 119): the seductive-cell promotion rule is coherent
as an anti-seduction filter (per-look false-promotion at a true-null
cell 0.007-0.024, at or below the nominal 2.5 percent, conservative
by construction) and NOT coherent as a pre-registered test on four
counts: the denominator ("the unconditional forward event share over
the same rows") is ambiguous between the pooled share (0.634, the
reading most faithful to the words), the climatology (0.441) and the
doc's own half-comparison (0.476), and the choice moves the promotion
floor by a factor of 3-4 in required rows; the motivating example
(0.476 vs 0.800) is not the comparison the rule performs; no operating
characteristics were declared for the only gate family that lacks
them; and its re-examination is uncorrected repeated looking, softened
only by its doc-action status. Proposal R-1 (pin the denominator to
the pooled reading, declare the table, optionally bind examinations to
milestones) sits in the ratification queue at ZERO STATUS and dies
whole on his one word of refusal. The main proof (57 of 104) is
untouched by any cell verdict by construction.

6.2 The sizing layer's two honesty verdicts (smh_sizing_v0.md, session
52, block 60): the armed-window reduction policy is DEAD (the
0.634/hazard arming doesn't cut the drawdown because the clock's
event is SIGMA-RELATIVE while equity drawdown mass is SIGMA-LEVEL -
lesson 15); the lever that worked descriptively on that sample, with
no decision attached, is vol targeting (max drawdown -0.384 vs -0.695,
a 45 percent reduction, with total return higher, +4.59 vs +3.92, at
mean exposure 0.79, Calmar 0.233 vs 0.119 - in-sample for its
conventions). The P5 sizing bridge is measured, descriptive,
pre-decision.

6.3 The design's small-n spine: n = 41 entries in ~20 years; every
design cell small; the direction splits doubly so; the single-print
intensity definition-made with its caveat frozen beside it; the
lone-print provenance window (12 Nov 2026, sessions 57-71) rather
than an exact session. The forward machinery exists precisely because
the design sample cannot be its own proof.

## 7. The hypothesis as one falsifiable structure

Assembled, the salient market hypothesis is this specific, scored-
in-advance claim set: under the frozen definitions (trigger cut
0.01739, event |r1| >= 2.5 sig_w within 12 weeks, KM clock), the
compression cross raises the 12-week first-event probability from
climatology 0.441 to 0.634, the structure recurs in ~a quarter, the
level and mean of the phenomenon are priced, and the dated-hazard
calibration record is the private residue. It is falsified if the
kill lines fire (K <= 3/8/19/31 at 10/20/40/60 rows, the binding
public test). It is reduced, not falsified, if the direction peeks
fail (door 3 stays timing-only; the residue is actuarial sizing
posture). It is economically hollowed, though not falsified as
prediction, if the event-structure test (Amendment 14, unsigned)
shows the dated hazard fully priced (the residue is
knowing-your-own-scoreboard: calibration as process quality, not
income - declared now so the result cannot be argued around later).
It is positively proved only by 57 of 104 resolved windows - nothing
less counts, no intermediate readout is over-read, and nothing in the
design sample counts at all.

## 8. What the hypothesis does NOT claim (the not-claimed section)

Not direction: the anticipated change is a significant MOVE, never a
signed one; the CP95 lower bound 0.443 is below one half; direction
arrives only through a promoting peek. Not edge: zero forward rows
have been scored; per the 1-in-8 honesty statement an edge, if one
appears, is indistinguishable from an unfalsified hypothesis with
good hygiene until the forward log separates it. Not profit: paper
trading only; no live claim exists or is claimed. Not certainty: no
dated instance is ever inevitable. Not a completed theory: the
forward log is empty by design and is the proof object; this draft is
the standing hypothesis, not the result.

## 9. On "new and first of market theory" (the owner's framing, handled
honestly)

The owner's stated final goal is a new, first-of-its-kind market
theory called the salient market hypothesis. Two honest statements,
so the goal is neither flattered nor quietly dropped. First: novelty
cannot be self-certified. The programme cannot verify that no prior
literature states this structure; the record's own knowledge base
(Bouchaud, O'Hara, the falsification-methods spine) is the closer, not
the wider, and the record already states that claims of novelty
without a literature sweep are speculation. What the programme CAN say
is structural: the hypothesis is stated with pre-declared kill lines,
a single positive-proofs statement, refused direction, and priced-
ness measurements integrated into the statement itself, which is a
stronger falsifiable structure than the descriptive frameworks it
refused (the received Elliott Wave). Second: the only admissible
proof of the goal, in the programme's own terms, is the forward
calibration record; a draft, however complete, is testimony, and the
observer corollary already established that testimony is not a
private advantage. The charter (section 9) further refuses the
quantum/singularity framing the owner used to describe the autonomy
that would build this: the claim structure of the final theory is
state-evolution with proof keys held by the owner, no more.
