# THE SALIENT MARKET HYPOTHESIS
## A Falsifiable Theory of Liquidity and Time on the Weekly Chart
### Thesis, version 1.0

Written 19 November 2026, session 131, at the owner's directive to write
the salient market hypothesis from scratch to finish. Status: THEORY-CLASS
DOCUMENTATION under autonomy charter v1, class T. ZERO STATUS until the
forward calibration record accrues rows. Every quantitative statement in
this thesis is a frozen design figure produced on unsealed pre-2021 weekly
NQ data and already recorded in the programme bundle; design statistics are
bookkeeping, never proof, and the proof object is the forward calibration
record alone, which is empty by design. The standing consolidation draft
(smh_theory_consolidation_v0.md, session 119) is cited throughout and not
rewritten; where this thesis paraphrases, the frozen source wording
governs. Kill criteria of every claim are stated with the claim.

The two load-bearing sentences of the programme, absolute and permanent:

The default assumption is NO EDGE. The burden of proof is absolute.

---

## Abstract

The salient market hypothesis (SMH) claims that a price series carries a
recurrent, measurable, salience-local timing structure. On the weekly grain
of a liquid equity-index future, one entry condition is measured to raise
the conditional probability of a significant single-week move within twelve
weeks from the unconditional climatology 0.441 [0.411, 0.471] to a measured
0.634 [0.487, 0.782], with disjoint confidence bands: when 8-week realized
volatility crosses DOWN into its compression region (a cross, not a dwell),
the 12-week first-event hazard of a significant week, defined as
|r1| >= 2.5 x sig_w of the decision week, rises materially above the
do-nothing baseline. The content of the signature is DURATION, a clock
anchored at the cross, not the entry conditions themselves. The structure
recurs actuarially, roughly 2.04 triggers and 1.29 anticipated events per
year on the design sample, with the full circle in medians about 29 weeks
end to end; no dated instance is ever inevitable. The public levels and
means of the phenomenon are PRICED: implied volatility tracks the clock
monotonically, and the variance risk premium survives the trigger, so the
hypothesis cannot rest on public-information mispricing. What remains
private, at the weekly grain, is the CALIBRATION RECORD, the act of
pre-registering a dated hazard and scoring it against realized outcomes.
The hypothesis is falsifiable in public by kill lines declared in advance
(3/8/19/31 resolved events at 10/20/40/60 scored forward rows) and by one
positive proof statement: at least 57 of 104 resolved compression 12-week
windows must resolve with the event. Zero forward rows have been scored.
Direction is refused until forward rows earn it. This thesis states the
hypothesis, its every constituent finding, its priced-ness measurements,
its proof protocol, its falsification record, its known defects, and what
it does not claim. It is a standing hypothesis, not a result.

---

# PART I. THE PROBLEM

## Chapter 1. The motivating question

A circus lion goes feral during a show. To the audience there were no
signs moments prior; to whoever assembled the conditions, the escape was
already overdetermined. The motivating intuition of this research
programme is that markets have moments of this shape: a significant move
arrives, and afterwards the crowd insists it was unheralded, while a
measurable state of the market had quietly made it materially more
likely. The question the programme set out to answer, in its owner's own
framing, is whether a set of recurring, effectively inevitable conditions
can be identified, close in time, that mark the beginning of a significant
directional impulse, before it arrives, across markets.

That framing contains three demands: inevitability, timing, and direction.
The programme's first and most consequential finding is a theorem about
that triple, and the entire shape of SMH is a consequence of it. The
present chapter states the problem; the next chapter states the theorem
that shapes it.

The problem, formalized, is four questions at a decision close:

1. WHEN is a big move likely? (the hazard question)
2. HOW CLOSE in time can we get to it? (the timeliness question)
3. WHICH WAY will it go? (the direction question)
4. WHAT DOES IT COST to be there? (the exploitability question)

Everything the programme measured is an answer to one of these four, a
measured refusal to answer one of them, or a measured statement that the
answer is priced and therefore worthless as an edge.

## Chapter 2. The refusal theorem

The certainty-timing-direction triangle, consolidated at sessions 36-37 of
the programme and formalized at session 30 (block 38), states: a PUBLIC
rule claiming all three of certainty, timing, and direction is priced
away. The logic parents recorded at its formalization are the
no-arbitrage and martingale machinery of Björk and Elliott-Kopp, the
no-trade theorems of O'Hara's microstructure canon, and the empirical
publication-decay evidence of McLean-Pontiff: whatever a public rule can
guarantee about a future price is arbitraged into the current price until
it guarantees nothing. Inevitability without timing is true and priced;
inevitability with both timing and direction collapses back into
statistics. There is no third door for a public rule. The refusal is a
theorem, not a mood.

The theorem was not accepted on faith; the programme tested the tradition
that claimed otherwise. Elliott Wave as received claimed all three
(certainty, timing, direction) and produced zero testable predictions,
which is why it was refused as theory and retained only as a descriptive
canvas layer for annotation. Volume Spread Analysis, the Wyckoff event
dictionaries, the Copsey cycle ladders: each was studied in full from the
programme's book collection, encoded where encodable, and subjected to the
same registration standard. None bypassed the theorem. The chapter of the
record that tests the AVFI family (Chapters 21-22 of Part VI) is the
demonstration that the programme tests even its owner's standing beliefs
exactly as directed and freezes the nulls.

## Chapter 3. What remains private

The observer corollary (session 32, block 40) is the constructive turn of
the refusal theorem: the only forecast that cannot be priced away is the
private one, existing in exactly three forms:

1. INFORMATION others lack;
2. SPEED others lack;
3. A CALIBRATION RECORD others have not measured.

SMH builds the third. The corollary carries a privacy caveat recorded as
interpretation, not measurement: privacy erodes on publication; the
protective privacy of the record is the pre-registration interval; and
what remains strictly private even after publication is the actuarial
posture the record licenses, sizing in proportion to the measured hazard.
The programme's measured door status, stated fully in Chapter 14:
information is CLOSED at this grain on levels and means (with one
sub-door pending on unsigned data-side drafts), speed is NOT HELD at the
weekly grain (order-flow branches are pre-registration only, blocked on
data), and calibration is UNDER CONSTRUCTION. The forward log, empty by
design, is the proof object.

---

# PART II. THE FORMAL STRUCTURE

## Chapter 4. Objects and layers

A theory of liquidity and time must keep its objects separate. The
programme's five-layer architecture, held everywhere in this thesis:

1. MARKET-STATE MEASUREMENT. Where is the series now? Descriptive,
   bookkeeping, never a claim. (sig_w, the region occupancy, the AVFI
   state.)
2. EVENT PROBABILITY / HAZARD. Given the state, how likely is the
   anticipated event within the observation horizon?
3. POTENTIAL PRECURSOR. Does anything add to the hazard beyond the state
   and the clock? (Tested covariates: null unless forward rows say
   otherwise.)
4. DIRECTIONAL EVIDENCE. Which way is the event signed? Refused until
   forward rows earn it; the anticipated change is a significant MOVE,
   never a signed one.
5. EXECUTION / RISK CONTEXT. What position, if any, does the hazard
   license, at what cost? Descriptive sizing only; paper trading only;
   nothing in this thesis is tradeable advice.

Conflating these layers is how theories launder themselves: a state
measurement dressed as a prediction; a direction guess dressed as a
hazard; a sizing preference dressed as a finding. Every statement in this
thesis names its layer.

## Chapter 5. The frozen definitions

The definitions below are frozen in the programme bundle and are quoted
from their frozen sources. sig_w is the 8-week trailing weekly log-return
standard deviation (ddof=1), including the current week.

THE TRIGGER (smh_hazard_v0.md, session 32). Compression entry at t:
sig_w(t) <= LOW (LOW = 0.01739, the frozen pre-2021 tercile cutpoint) AND
sig_w(t-1) > LOW. A cross, not a dwell. True crosses pre-2021: n = 41.
The load-bearing part is the crossing week: the trigger is the cross into
the compression region, never a residence in it.

THE EVENT (same source). The significant 1-week event: |r1(t+1)| >= 2.5
x sig_w(t), first occurrence within 12 weeks of the entry. The event is
defined as a MOVE, never a signed move; direction is not part of the
event definition. The realized design anatomy (smh_event_anatomy_v0.md,
session 39): the median event overshoots its own bar by 1.41x (maximum
2.63x), so the median significant week is a ~3.5-sigma week. The median
dwell of the compression spell is 4 weeks; the entry-anchored clock and
the spell-anchored abort condition were reconciled by the
competing-risks decomposition.

THE CLOCK (sessions 32-38). The duration machinery: entry-anchored
Kaplan-Meier first-event clocks per region. The clock is anchored at the
entry cross and does NOT require the region to be maintained inside the
window. The measured content verdict, from the Cox v0.1 null
(smh_strategy_v0.md rung 8): the hazard content of the compression
signature is DURATION, honestly the pooled level and not even the slope,
not the entry conditions.

THE ARMED CLOCK (smh_sizing_v0.md, session 52, block 60). ARMED(t) holds
when week t falls in the 12 weeks after any compression entry t' <= t-1.
A new cross re-arms; overlapping windows do not stack; the armed clock
runs the full 12 weeks with no early termination at an event, because
cutting it short would be a second, untested policy. The armed clock is a
locked register in the record. Its measured verdict as a sizing lever is
recorded honestly in Chapter 24: the armed-window reduction policy is
DEAD.

THE REGIONS. The same sig_w axis, cut by the frozen terciles into
compression (LOW), mid, and stress (HIGH): the salience gradient of
Chapter 8 is measured across these three entry regions plus the
any-week climatology.

## Chapter 6. The hypothesis, stated formally

H-SMH, the salient market hypothesis, is this specific claim set under
the frozen definitions of Chapter 5:

C1 (OCCURRENCE). At a compression cross t, the conditional probability
that the event occurs within the 12-week window, computed by the
entry-anchored KM clock, exceeds the any-decision-week climatology, with
disjoint confidence bands. Design figures: 0.634 [0.487, 0.782] from 26
of 41 entries, versus 0.441 [0.411, 0.471] from 1050 decision weeks.

C2 (CONTENT). The information content of the signature is duration: the
clock anchored at the cross carries the hazard; the entry conditions
beyond the cross itself carry none measured beyond it.

C3 (LOCALITY). The salience is edge-local: only the compression cross
separates from climatology with disjoint bands; the mid region does not
clearly separate, and the stress region sits below climatology after its
own events pass (the suppression side, Chapter 8).

C4 (RECURRENCE). The structure is actuarial: triggers recur indefinitely
at the design rate (about 2.04 per year; about 1.29 entry-conditioned
events per year); the full circle in medians from cross to next cross is
about 29 weeks end to end. No dated instance is ever inevitable.

C5 (PRICED-NESS). The levels and the mean of the phenomenon are priced
(Chapter 12): the signature is level-cheap but the crowd's calm at
compression entries is still overpriced calm; the variance risk premium
survives the trigger at a thinner level.

C6 (THE PRIVATE RESIDUE). What the hypothesis leaves over, at the weekly
grain, is the calibration record: a pre-registered, dated, scored
forward log whose actuarial value is the measured license it provides
(the posture of readiness at the cross), not a mispricing.

Every clause carries its kill machinery (Chapters 15-19). None of them is
currently proved: the forward log is empty by design, and the design
figures above are bookkeeping on the unsealed pre-2021 sample.

DESIGN-ONLY CROSS-MARKET NOTE. The same rank-transferred rule, zero
re-tuning, measured on the design windows of three further markets
(smh_crossmarket_stageA_v0; descriptive Stage A bookkeeping, sealed
windows untouched): GOLD cum12 0.617 [0.478, 0.756] versus 0.481, gap
+0.136; NIKKEI 0.562 [0.422, 0.703] versus 0.374, gap +0.188; DAX 0.680
[0.551, 0.809] versus 0.465, gap +0.215. The gap sign is positive in
every market tested, each within the NQ grid's own range. These are
bookkeeping figures; the cross-market 2021+ windows are sealed and single
open, only on the owner's dated go.

---

# PART III. THE MEASURED STRUCTURE
### (design sample, unsealed pre-2021 weekly NQ; bookkeeping, never proof)

## Chapter 7. The trigger and the event

The compression cross fired n = 41 times in roughly 20 years of
unsealed pre-2021 weekly data. Of these entries, 26 resolved with the
significant-week event inside the 12-week window: occurrence 0.634,
Greenwood band [0.487, 0.782]. The matched climatology, over 1050
decision weeks, is 0.441 [0.411, 0.471]. The bands are disjoint; the
gap is +0.193. The median lag from the entry to the first event is 5
weeks.

The corrected-table log is part of the frozen provenance: an off-by-one
risk-set bug in the first KM table (343 versus 327 person-weeks) was
caught by the standing consistency check and corrected in the record by
append. This thesis reports the corrected figures.

The event anatomy (session 39): the median event overshoots its own
2.5-sigma threshold by 1.41x (maximum 2.63x). The anticipated "big move"
therefore means, concretely and measurably: a threshold-crossing single
week of central size about 3.5 sigma, not a point target and not a
cluster by construction.

## Chapter 8. The salience gradient

The 12-week first-event cumulative, by entry region (smh_mid_clock_v0.md,
session 38, block 46): compression (n = 41) 0.634; mid (n = 66) 0.470;
any week (n = 1050) 0.441; stress (n = 33) 0.273. The gradient is
monotone, and THE SALIENCE IS EDGE-LOCAL: only the compression cross
separates from climatology with disjoint bands.

The record's compressed form of the finding: above climatology at the
cross, below after the print. The post-print window is suppressed: its
12-week cum is 0.231 [0.069, 0.393] against climatology 0.441,
disjoint-band. This is the salience machine's own shape: the hazard
rises entering compression, realizes at the print, and falls quiet.

## Chapter 9. The capture ladder

Timeliness is a measured margin, not a felt skill
(smh_entry_theorem_v0.md, sessions 37-38):

- Enter at the cross: 0.634.
- Wait 4 quiet weeks, then enter: 0.500.
- Wait 6 quiet weeks: 0.348.

Waiting gains nothing the clock does not already condition on, forfeits
the front-loaded mass, and races the spell's own mortality (median dwell
4 weeks). The compression cross is the measured closest approach to the
event at the weekly grain; the 12-week protocol window is the measured
location of the asymmetry, not arbitrary bookkeeping. Finer timing than
the cross needs intraday data the programme does not hold; the L2
runbook is written and its download is the owner's hands.

## Chapter 10. The cycle

The trigger recurs in about a quarter (smh_aftermath_v0.md, session 41).
The next compression cross follows the event at a median of 15 weeks
(one return censored at the sample end); the return to the trigger is
FASTER than the arrival of a fresh stress spell (median 23.5 weeks). The
full circle, in medians: cross, then the print at 5 weeks; then, within
the quarter (17 of 26 events), the stress region about 3 weeks later;
then the de-stress clock (6 weeks from a stress entry to its cum 0.636
by 12 weeks); then the next cross, 15 weeks from the print. End to end,
about 29 weeks.

The modal episode is a single print against every yardstick: 22 of 26
design windows hold exactly one significant week and no further one.
The clustering intensity is definition-made (the median inter-arrival
gap between clustered prints is about 18.5 weeks against the event's
own drifting endogenous yardstick), and that caveat is frozen beside
the number rather than argued away.

Actuarial rates (smh_strategy_v0.md rungs 7/9; smh_dossier_v0.md):
about 2.04 compression triggers and about 1.29 entry-conditioned events
per year on the design sample; region occupancy 27.5 / 30.6 / 41.9
percent. ROWS ARE STABLE, YEARS ARE NOT: the capture fraction moved
only 0.647 versus 0.625 across decade halves while the trigger RATE
moved by a factor of 4.5 (regime range 0.7-3.2 per year). The proof
schedule in Part V therefore counts rows, never years.

## Chapter 11. Robustness

The design definition is deliberately NOT the grid optimum. The 11-cell
definition-elasticity audit (smh_robustness_v0.md, family_registry_v0.md)
found the compression gap positive in every cell (gaps 0.106-0.222),
so the frozen definition survives its own perturbation neighborhood
without being the best cell in it; the standing rule against post-null
re-tuning is what keeps this audit honest. The cross-market transfer of
Chapter 6, with zero re-tuning, is the second robustness leg. The
third leg is negative: the second trigger family (stress-side entry)
was measured weaker (Chapter 15's honest reading) and its null risks
refused re-tuning exactly as the AVFI nulls were.

---

# PART IV. PRICED-NESS
### (what the market already sees; smh_unseen_v0.md, session 40, block 48)

## Chapter 12. Level-cheap, mean-priced

THE LEVEL. The compression signature sits in cheap volatility territory:
the median VXN at compression entries is 16.47 versus 20.14 across all
pre-2021 weeks; entries sit at the 25.8th percentile of VXN's own
distribution (mid entries the 46.3rd, stress entries the 67.4th; by
region occupancy, compression weeks the 22.5th, mid 42.4th, stress
77.7th). Implied volatility follows the clock, monotonically. The
market is not blind to the state.

THE MEAN. The strong unseen-ness claim, that the market fails to price
the coming expansion, is REFUTED at the mean in the design sample: the
variance risk premium survives the trigger, +3.71 vol points at entries
(positive at 73.2 percent of entries) versus +4.55 across all weeks
(76.7 percent); forward 12-week realized volatility at entries is 13.96
versus 17.22 all weeks. The crowd's calm is still overpriced calm,
thinner premium, no inversion. Frozen consequence: unseen is the wrong
word for cheap. The edge claim, if any, cannot rest on options
mispricing at this grain; it rests on the unmeasured event-structure
sub-door (whether the specific DATED hazard priced by the options term
structure differs from the clock; pending unsigned data-side drafts and
the owner's data-source choice) and on the forward calibration record.

## Chapter 13. The priced-calm covariate split

One covariate split was measured at the owner's direction and is
registered, flagged, untrusted (smh_priced_calm_v0.md, session 43): at
the frozen cutpoint VXN 16.47 (the pre-2021 entry median, which makes
forward classification deterministic), the residual-fear half of
compression entries (VXN above the cut) shows 0.800 [0.625, 0.975]
against the deep-calm half's 0.476 [0.263, 0.690]. The split is
era-confounded (the halves separate the high-VXN decades from the
low-VXN decades) and is carried in the record as a seductive cell, not
a finding: it becomes examinable only at the forward milestones, under
the cell protocol of Chapter 19, and never before.

## Chapter 14. The three doors, measured

Door 1, INFORMATION: CLOSED at this grain on levels and means. The
event-structure sub-door is unmeasured and rides the owner's unsigned
data-source decision. Door 2, SPEED: NOT HELD. The weekly OHLCV
programme holds no order flow; the L2 and order-flow branches are
pre-registration-only and blocked on the download; a separate judgment
verdict (block 36) refused any speed claim on proxy data. Door 3,
CALIBRATION: UNDER CONSTRUCTION. The empty forward log is the proof
object. The hypothesis's private content is door 3, and it is
epistemic before it is economic: a verifiable record of conditional
mastery whose economic content exists only if the block-93 gaps close
(direction, event-structure priced-ness, post-cost exploitability, and
the forward rows themselves).

---

# PART V. THE PROOF PROTOCOL
### (smh_gates_v0.md, smh_proof_criteria_v0.md, sessions 43-46)

## Chapter 15. The kill lines

The occurrence claim C1 (design p0 = 0.634, 26 of 41) dies in public if
the forward record shows K resolved-event rows at or below its kill
line at a milestone: K <= 3 at n = 10 scored rows; K <= 8 at n = 20;
K <= 19 at n = 40; K <= 31 at n = 60. The rules are sticky,
milestone-exact: rows of the other kind never leak in, unresolved rows
never count. Per-gate false-kill (a true 0.634 machine dying at that
gate): 0.0335 / 0.0282 / 0.0290 / 0.0415. Power against the
climatology-measured world: 0.2855 / 0.4465 / 0.7243 / 0.9046. The
falsificationist's fee is roughly 3-4 percent per gate; that price is
the point, not the defect.

The stress-side de-stress claim (p0 = 0.636, 21 of 33 design) lands on
the SAME thresholds, honestly weaker against a coin (power 0.1719 /
0.2517 / 0.4373 / 0.6506), with no asymmetric tuning applied: the
second trigger family is recorded as the weaker clue and never
re-tuned to look better.

## Chapter 16. Cumulative honesty

Two frozen statements prevent the programme from celebrating either
survival or death without the schedule's own arithmetic. First, a TRUE
machine following this gate schedule dies somewhere in the four gates
roughly one run in eight (about 0.126, independence approximation; the
gates share rows). Second, the design battery of claims was expected,
by its own composition, to produce roughly one chance survivor. The
nulls of Part VI are the working half of this honesty: they are the
record of the battery's other members dying where they fell.

## Chapter 17. Calibration expectations, declared in advance

If the design is right, SMH-forecast rows should cost 0.6568 nats per
resolved window (Brier 0.2320) against climatology-forecast rows' 0.7319
(Brier 0.2693), an expected log-score edge of 0.0751 nats per row. If
climatology is right, the SMH rows lose 0.0766 (0.7628 versus 0.6862),
symmetric by construction: the scoring rule cannot favour the
hypothesis on expectation unless the outcomes actually do. Readouts are
judged at 26, 52, and 104 resolved windows.

## Chapter 18. The positive proof, and the four outcomes

The positive proof is exactly one statement: at least 57 of 104
resolved compression 12-week windows resolve with the event (the
stricter of the two registered rules). Power 0.971; false-pass 0.018.
Nothing less counts; no intermediate readout is over-read; nothing in
the design sample counts at all.

Four outcomes are kept distinct, so that no result can be argued
around after the fact.

1. FALSIFIED: a kill line fires. The claim dies; the obituary is
   written; nothing is re-tuned.
2. REDUCED: the direction peeks fail. Door 3 stays timing-only; the
   residue is actuarial sizing posture.
3. HOLLOWED (economically, not predictively): the event-structure test
   shows the dated hazard fully priced. The residue is
   knowing-your-own-scoreboard: calibration as process quality, not
   income.
4. PROVED: 57 of 104. Nothing above, below, or around that number.

## Chapter 19. Direction peeks and the seductive-cell register

Direction is refused until forward rows earn it. Two peeks are
pre-declared, counted in resolved forward first EVENTS (not rows):
promotion only at up >= 27/40 at 40 events (Clopper-Pearson 95 percent
lower bound above one half) or up >= 39/60 at 60 events; a down-bias
annotation at up <= 13/40 or <= 21/60; otherwise direction stays
refused. The refusal's design basis: 17 of 26 first events were up,
CP95 [0.443, 0.828], the lower bound below one half. A significant
MOVE, never a signed one.

Three seductive cells are registered with frozen definitions, flagged,
untrusted: momentum12 <= 0 at entry (design 6/7);
reversal_down macro state at entry (design 9/12); the priced-calm
residual-fear half (VXN > 16.47 at entry, design 16/20). Cells are
examined only at n >= 10 forward rows, promoted only on a CP95 lower
bound exceeding the unconditional forward event share, never automated,
counted silently below the floor. The critique that keeps this rule in
its place is Chapter 23's second defect: the promotion rule is coherent
as an anti-seduction filter and not coherent as a pre-registered test
(a proposal to repair it, R-1, sits in the ratification queue at zero
status and dies whole on the owner's one word of refusal). The main
proof of Chapter 18 is untouched by any cell verdict by construction.

## Chapter 20. Timing honesty

At the design trigger rate (2.04 triggers, 1.29 entry-conditioned
events per year), the occurrence gates arrive in roughly 5, 10, 20, and
29 YEARS, and the 104-window readout in roughly half a century,
halved if both row windows count. The readout schedule is roughly 13,
25, and 51 years for the intermediate calibration judgments. The
schedule counts rows, never years, precisely because of Chapter 10's
regime instability: a hot regime opens the first gate in about 3
years, a cold one nearer 15. The cross-market forward stream (Amendment
9, signed and first-firing after this thesis's date) is the only
instrument that can open gates before NQ's own clock, and it runs
under its own registered protocol. The thesis states this horizon
plainly because hiding it would turn the protocol into theatre.

---

# PART VI. THE FALSIFICATION RECORD
### (what died, and why that is the asset)

## Chapter 21. The nulls before SMH

The hypothesis of Part II was built only after a full programme of
candidate edges had been tested honestly and killed. The record, in
brief:

- RULE SET A (AVFI state system): killed. In-sample Sharpe -0.115,
  worse than the random-entry control; validation 0.420, equal to
  random, at one-eighth of buy-and-hold upside. The registered
  |sp_z|>=1 variant was structurally vacuous: zero trades ever.
- H1/H2 (AVFI conditional structures): not supported.
- STAGE 6A FAMILY (27-combination AVFI grid): fails White's Reality
  Check. Family best 0.58, RC p = 0.11. Every AVFI variant fails
  individually. The only variant significant in both windows was the
  12-week momentum control, consistent with the generic
  trend-following prior, not with AVFI.
- H8 (AVFI flow-fade divergence at tops, Amendment 3, signed and
  executed exactly as directed): n = 32, WRONG-SIGNED, p 0.79 at the
  primary 4-week horizon. Volume divergence does not call tops in the
  future tense. H8 bottom arm: 0 events, vacuous.
- H9 (effort-vs-result climax): n = 3 events in 26 years; p 0.082 at
  the primary horizon, right direction, fails alpha, no support
  claimed. Family max-t 0.089. The frozen defaults are never re-tuned
  post-null.
- AMENDMENTS 4 AND 5 (regime-quality and terminal-detector confirmatory
  tests): WITHDRAWN UNEXECUTED after their pre-sign-off power readouts.
  The H10a test as drafted was invalid (measured false-positive 7-16
  percent against nominal 5: the predictors are near-integrated, and
  within-sample resampling cannot size them); the validated
  circular-shift revision reaches only 21-29 percent power at
  plausible effects. The H11a test is correctly sized but only detects
  a ~1.25-sigma effect at 80 percent power; meaningful 0.1-0.3 sigma
  effects are detected 7-13 percent of the time. Both withdrawn rather
  than run underpowered.
- THE SPZ COVARIATE NULL: at-entry sp_z adds nothing to the hazard, as
  pre-declared.
- THE HOLDOUT BATTERY (Amendment 1/2, the single open of the sealed
  2021+ window, run once on the owner's explicit go): NOTHING PASSES.
  Rule set T (12-week momentum long/flat): Sharpe 0.575, bootstrap p
  0.069 against the pre-committed 0.05; false at the margin. Rule set
  U3: 0.418, p 0.116; false. H7 (bear-capitulation bounce):
  UNTESTABLE, its state extinct in the modern era (occupancy 0 against
  a gate of 10). The holdout is now SPENT and is never evaluated again
  under any circumstance, even descriptively.

## Chapter 22. The lessons the nulls taught

Three of the record's fifteen-plus lessons carry the theory's technical
spine. Lesson 11/13: near-integrated weekly series (lag-1 of order
0.92+) make within-sample resampling invalid; persistence-preserving
nulls (the Theiler circular-shift family) or validated event-study
machinery are the only lawful tests, and the H10a withdrawal is the
worked demonstration. Lo's Sharpe statistics: annualized headline
Sharpe is banned programme-wide; per-period statistics only. DSR and
PBO: holdout repetition spends holdouts; N = 1 at the moment of
specification is what the signed-dated-amendment rule guarantees; the
~1,000-observation holdout minimum frames the weekly grain honestly.
The battery taught two more: magnitude can replicate while
significance fails (T's 0.575 holdout against 0.571 unsealed, missing
at exactly the 43-percent-power-predicted rate), and insurance is
only worth its premium in the insured regime (U3's drawdown control
underperformed exactly when the crisis regime it hedged against was
absent).

## Chapter 23. Why the record is the asset

A theory's credibility is the set of claims it refused to make cheaply.
SMH arrives holding: a validation spine adopted before its own tests
(family max-t for variants, SPA with all three bounds for family
searches, BH with the BY correction for independent shortlists, DSR
with honest trial counts for final picks); a spent holdout that will
never be re-spent; nulls frozen rather than re-tuned; two underpowered
amendments withdrawn unexecuted rather than run for the record; and an
owner standing belief (AVFI tops/bottoms) tested exactly as directed
and recorded as a belief, not a finding. The default assumption that
there is no edge, and the burden of proof, are the two load-bearing
sentences precisely because everything in this Part was built under
them. The falsification record IS the asset; the hypothesis is the
instrument that keeps the record growing.

---

# PART VII. KNOWN DEFECTS
### (stated inside the theory, not in footnotes)

## Chapter 24. The defects

1. THE SMALL-N SPINE. 41 compression entries in about 20 years; every
   design cell is small; the direction splits doubly so; the
   single-print intensity is definition-made with its caveat frozen
   beside it; the lone-print grid's exact provenance is a stated
   session-window rather than a single session. The forward machinery
   exists precisely because the design sample cannot be its own
   proof.

2. THE CELL-PROMOTION CRITIQUE. The seductive-cell promotion rule is
   coherent as an anti-seduction filter (per-look false-promotion at a
   true-null cell 0.007-0.024, at or below the nominal 2.5 percent)
   and NOT coherent as a pre-registered test on four counts: its
   denominator is ambiguous between three readings that move the
   promotion floor by a factor of 3-4 in required rows; its motivating
   example is not the comparison the rule performs; it lacks declared
   operating characteristics; and its re-examination schedule is
   uncorrected repeated looking. Proposal R-1 (pin the denominator,
   declare the table) sits in the ratification queue at zero status.
   The main proof is untouched by any cell verdict by construction.

3. THE SIZING LAYER'S VERDICTS. The armed-window reduction policy is
   DEAD: the 0.634-class hazard does not cut the measured drawdown,
   because the clock's event is SIGMA-RELATIVE while equity drawdown
   mass is SIGMA-LEVEL (lesson 15). The lever that worked
   descriptively on the same sample, with no decision attached, is
   volatility targeting (max drawdown -0.384 versus -0.695, a 45
   percent reduction, with total return higher, +4.59 versus +3.92, at
   mean exposure 0.79; Calmar 0.233 versus 0.119; in-sample for its
   conventions). The sizing bridge is measured, descriptive,
   pre-decision. No statement in this thesis licenses a live position.

4. NOVELTY IS NOT SELF-CERTIFIABLE. The programme cannot verify that
   no prior literature states this structure; the record's own
   knowledge base, deep in microstructure, econometrics, and
   falsification methodology, is evidence of coverage, not of
   novelty. What the programme CAN say is structural: the hypothesis
   is stated with pre-declared kill lines, a single positive-proof
   statement, refused direction, and priced-ness measurements
   integrated into the statement itself, which is a stronger
   falsifiable structure than the descriptive frameworks it refused.
   A "first of market" claim, if it is ever earned, is earned by the
   forward record, not declared by a document.

---

# PART VIII. WHAT SMH DOES NOT CLAIM

Not direction. The anticipated change is a significant MOVE, never a
signed one; the CP95 lower bound 0.443 on the design split is below one
half; direction arrives only through a promoting peek (Chapter 19).

Not edge. Zero forward rows have been scored. Per the one-in-eight
honesty statement, an edge, if one appears, is indistinguishable from
an unfalsified hypothesis with good hygiene until the forward log
separates it (Chapter 16).

Not profit. Paper trading only. No live claim exists or is claimed.

Not certainty. No dated instance is ever inevitable. Inevitability is
actuarial: the trigger recurs; the event follows more often than
climatology; no weekend is promised.

Not entry rules beyond the cross. The compression cross is the measured
closest approach at the weekly grain; the covariate nulls of Part VI
are the record of every attempted refinement that failed.

Not a completed theory. The forward log is empty by design and is the
proof object. This thesis is the standing hypothesis, not the result.

---

# PART IX. THE ROAD

The proof object fills at the Sunday cadence: forward rows are scored
as decision weeks resolve, the occurrence gates open at 10, 20, 40, and
60 scored rows, the first gate is the binding next milestone, and the
cross-market macro stream (Amendment 9, first firing scheduled after
this thesis's date) is the only instrument that can open gates before
NQ's own clock. What the owner's hands can compress: the options
term-structure data-source decision (the event-structure sub-door of
Chapter 12); the broker/CME 1999-2000 export (the volume/Hawkes layer);
the L2 download (the speed door's pre-registration branch and the
intraday timing that Chapter 9 proves the weekly grain cannot give);
the unsigned drafts on the signature stack; the shelfed amendments.
What no hands can compress: the rows themselves. They arrive as the
market resolves them, or the theory dies at its kill lines first, and
either outcome is recorded.

The programme's definition of what it would mean for the owner's goal
to be met is precisely this: a theory stated so it can fail in public,
a record that scores it in public, and the refusal to claim anything
the record has not earned. The default assumption is NO EDGE. The
burden of proof is absolute.

---

# APPENDIX A. THE FROZEN LEXICON (the ten terms)

The programme's ten frozen terms, with definitions and origin sessions
(smh_lexicon_v0.md, session 117, block 120):

1. TRIGGER: the compression cross. sig_w(t) <= 0.01739 AND
   sig_w(t-1) > 0.01739. A cross, not a dwell. (session 32)
2. EVENT: |r1| >= 2.5 x sig_w of the decision week, first occurrence
   within 12 weeks. A MOVE, never a signed one. (session 32)
3. CLOCK: entry-anchored Kaplan-Meier first-event clocks per region;
   the clock does not require the region to persist. Content verdict:
   DURATION, the pooled level, not the slope. (sessions 32-38)
4. SALIENCE GRADIENT: compression 0.634 > mid 0.470 > any 0.441 >
   stress 0.273, monotone, edge-local. (session 38, block 46)
5. QUARTER RULE: the next cross follows the print at a median 15
   weeks; the return is faster than a fresh stress spell. (session 41)
6. ARMED CLOCK: the 12 weeks after any compression entry; re-arms on a
   new cross; no stacking; no early termination. Sizing verdict: the
   armed-window reduction is DEAD. (session 52, block 60)
7. LONE PRINT: the modal episode is a single significant week against
   every yardstick; the clustering intensity is definition-made, with
   the endogenous-yardstick caveat frozen beside it. (sessions 57-71
   window; exact session honestly not re-derived)
8. KILL LINES: 3/8/19/31 resolved events at 10/20/40/60 scored rows;
   sticky, milestone-exact; the one-in-eight statement rides with
   them. (sessions 43-44)
9. CAPTURE LADDER: 0.634 at the cross, 0.500 after 4 quiet weeks,
   0.348 after 6. Timeliness is a measured margin. (sessions 37-38)
10. PRICED-CALM: level-cheap (median VXN 16.47 at entries versus 20.14
    all weeks) and mean-priced (VRP +3.71 at entries versus +4.55);
    unseen is the wrong word for cheap. (sessions 40-43)

# SMH Transitions v0 (the dwell and transition layer: the clock machine completed)

Status: DESIGN BOOKKEEPING on the UNSEALED pre-2021 sample only (rule-set-V class). Date filter date < 2021-01-01 throughout; holdout 2021+ never touched. Built 12 Nov 2026, session 35. This document completes the measured skeleton of the SMH's state layer and clock layer: occupancy durations, the region transition machine, the AVFI state machine, and their joint occupancy. Same discipline as the other SMH design docs: no inferential claims, no forward statistics, no trades.

Bases, stated explicitly (they differ by conditioning and must not be mixed): 1050 region-labeled decision weeks (sig_w available; 8 leading weeks have no sig_w); 798 state-labeled weeks (AVFI state available; the state series begins later than price history); 41 compression entries; 33 stress entries. The clock study's n = 794 decision weeks was its own conditioning (state-labeled subset); the difference is definitional, not an inconsistency.

## 1. The clock region machine (dwell and transition)

Dwell distributions of region spells (consecutive weeks in one region):

| region | spells | weeks | median dwell | mean dwell | max dwell |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| compression (sig_w <= 0.01739) | 41 | 289 | 4 | 7.0 | 29 |
| mid | 66 | 321 | 3 | 4.9 | 16 |
| stress (sig_w > 0.02697) | 33 | 440 | 8 | 13.3 | 86 |

Consistency anchors: compression spells 41 = compression entries 41; stress spells 33 = stress entries 33. A spell begins with a cross by construction; both counting methods agree.

Transition matrix (row region -> next region spell, counts and row fractions):

| from | to mid | to compression | to stress |
|---|---|---|---|
| compression | 38 (0.93) | - | 3 (0.07) |
| mid | - | 37 (0.56) | 29 (0.44) |
| stress | 28 (0.88) | 4 (0.12) | - |

Readings, honestly:

1. THE ASYMMETRY OF THE MACHINE: stress dwells twice as long as compression (mean 13.3 vs 7.0 weeks, median 8 vs 4, max 86 vs 29) and occupies the most calendar time of any region (440 of 1050 weeks, 42 percent). Volatility spikes are sticky; volatility troughs are brief. This is law 1 (persistence then mean reversion) measured in TIME UNITS, and it is asymmetric: the machine climbs down from stress slowly and leaves compression quickly.
2. THE NO-WARNING PATH IS REAL BUT RARE: compression jumps directly to stress in 3 of 41 exits (0.07). Those are the events with no mid-region warning - the compression entry whose first significant week IS the stress entry. The other 93 percent pass through mid first, which is why the compression KM table has a rising early hazard but its cumulative probability takes weeks to build.
3. MID IS THE EXCHANGE: mid dwells only 3 weeks (median) and splits its exits 0.56 back to compression, 0.44 up to stress. It is a decision region, not a resting region.
4. Stress decompresses directly to compression in 4 of 33 exits (0.12); it almost always steps down through mid first (0.88).

## 2. The AVFI state machine (occupancy and transition)

Dwell distributions of state spells (798 state-labeled weeks):

| state | spells | weeks | median dwell | mean dwell | max dwell |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| momentum_up | 14 | 289 | 10 | 20.6 | 86 |
| reversal_down | 13 | 386 | 11 | 29.7 | 114 |
| momentum_down | 3 | 97 | 25 | 32.3 | 65 |
| reversal_up | 4 | 26 | 5 | 6.5 | 15 |

State transition matrix (row state -> next state):

| from | to momentum_up | to reversal_down | to momentum_down | to reversal_up |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| momentum_up | - | 13 (1.00) | 0 | 0 |
| reversal_down | 11 (0.85) | - | 2 (0.15) | 0 |
| momentum_down | 0 | 0 | - | 3 (1.00) |
| reversal_up | 3 (0.75) | 0 | 1 (0.25) | - |

Readings, honestly (all cells small-n; 3 to 14 spells per row; flag everything):

1. THE ALTERNATION ELLIOTT NAMED IS THE TRANSITION STRUCTURE, MEASURED: up-trends hand to down-reversals in 13 of 13 observed spells; down-trends (momentum_down, only 3 spells) hand to up-reversals in 3 of 3; reversal_down hands back to momentum_up in 11 of 13. The alternation is real and nearly deterministic IN TRANSITION. What Elliott never had is the second half of the same table: the TIMING is not deterministic (reversal_down dwells a median 11 weeks, mean 29.7, max 114 - the handoff has no fixed schedule), and the rare state is real: reversal_up occupied 26 weeks in twenty years (median dwell 5). The wave count said "five then three"; the machine says "however long, then the measured successor, usually".
2. THE RARE-STATE QUOTA CONFIRMS THE H9 VERDICT INDEPENDENTLY: reversal_up (climax) has 4 spells and 26 weeks pre-2021 - the anecdote quota the battery already found (n = 3 climax events) is an occupancy fact, not a sampling accident. Any climax-based rule is structurally starved of data in this market at this horizon.
3. STATE AND CLOCK ARE NEARLY ORTHOGONAL (the joint table, section 3): the AVFI states appear in all three clock regions with no dominant diagonal. AVFI cycles while the clock ticks; neither explains the other. This is the regime-descriptor verdict, now visible in occupancy units.

## 3. Joint occupancy (state x region, weeks; 798 state-labeled weeks)

| state | compression | mid | stress |
|---|---|---|---|
| momentum_up | 155 | 74 | 60 |
| reversal_down | 91 | 149 | 146 |
| momentum_down | 7 | 28 | 62 |
| reversal_up | 12 | 13 | 1 |

Reading: momentum_up is the compression-heavy state (155 of its 289 weeks); momentum_down lives mostly in stress (62 of 97 - downtrends are high-vol); reversal_down spreads evenly. No cell concentrates: the state does not forecast the clock region and the clock region does not forecast the state.

## 4. What this completes, and what remains

With this document, every layer of the framework reachable with current data has measured design tables: the laws (prior programme results), the clock (region dwell/transitions here; both edge clocks in smh_hazard_v0.md and smh_stress_clock_v0.md; the quarter rule), the states (occupancy/transitions/joint here), the event (both KM clocks), covariates (the Cox v0.1 null verdict), the direction cells (flagged, unmodeled), and the falsification instruments (forward protocol, smh_forecast.py, smh_score.py, FTEP). The design layer is COMPLETE. What remains is exactly two classes: FORWARD ACCUMULATION (the protocol's logged rows, scored as windows complete - nothing in this document can be updated from post-2020 data ever) and DATA-BLOCKED LAYERS (the volume clock and Hawkes self-excitation need the broker/CME export; the unseen-ness test needs the VXN benchmark). No theory work remains that would not be decoration on unmeasured speculation; the honest next increments are episodes and data.

## 5. Caveats, frozen

1. All cells are small-n (3 to 66 spells); the state machine rows are the smallest (3 to 14); every fraction carries its count beside it for a reason.
2. Spell transitions count consecutive spells; adjacent spells of the same state separated by a NaN week merge incorrectly if NaNs occurred mid-series (checked: the state series has no interior NaN gaps; the region series has 8 leading NaNs only).
3. The transition matrices are descriptive counts on one instrument's pre-2021 history; no ergodic claim, no stationarity claim, no fitted Markov model. Fitting one would be forward-scored speculation at this n.
4. All numbers are unsealed pre-2021 design bookkeeping. No claim, no test, no trade.

# SMH Unseen-ness Layer v0: is the compression signature priced?

Status: DESIGN BOOKKEEPING on the UNSEALED pre-2021 sample only (rule-set-V class). Date filter date < 2021-01-01 throughout; holdout 2021+ SPENT and never touched. Built 12 Nov 2026, session 40, PROGRESS block 48, on Andrea's "Continue building". This layer resolves the standing data need recorded in docs/smh_hazard_v0.md section 6 (an implied-vol benchmark to test whether the compression signature is genuinely unseen or already priced) and sharpens the observer corollary's honest content. Nothing here is an inferential claim, a test, or a trade.

Benchmark provenance: data/vxn_weekly.csv, built by research/stage0v_vxn.py (Yahoo v8 daily ^VXN from 2001-01-23 and ^VIX from 2000-01-03, W-FRI aggregation; SHA256 and provenance in docs/provenance_manifest.md). Instrument: research/smh_unseen.py (status = the unseen-ness card; selftest recomputes and asserts every constant), wired into verify_all.sh as its 20th check. The fetch includes post-2021 quotes per endpoint behaviour, exactly as the canonical NQ file does; every design number below filters date < 2021-01-01.

## 1. The three measured answers

The question decomposes into three, and the design sample answers each differently. Coverage first: all 41 compression entries carry a VXN quote (the first pre-2021 compression entry post-dates VXN's birth); 32 of 33 stress entries do (the one missing entry precedes 2001-02).

### 1a. LEVEL: options are cheap at the trigger

Median VXN at compression entries: 16.47, versus 20.14 across all pre-2021 weeks. In its own-distribution percentile: compression entries sit at the 25.8th percentile, mid entries at the 46.3rd, stress entries at the 67.4th. By region occupancy the same monotone tracking: compression weeks 22.5th percentile, mid 42.4th, stress 77.7th. Two consequences, frozen:

1. The compression signature is LEVEL-cheap. The options market is calm exactly where the SMH clock is armed: the entry condition coincides with the cheapest quartile of implied vol. Whatever the crowd is pricing at the cross, it is not drama. This is the first measured support for the "trade no one sees" intuition: in level terms, the trigger is not an alert anyone is watching.
2. IMPLIED VOL TRACKS THE CLOCK. The percentile gradient across regions is monotone and large (22.5 -> 42.4 -> 77.7). The options market sees the same three-region machine the SMH measures; its read is correlated, not blind. So the SMH clock is not private information; it is public structure that options prices already follow.

### 1b. MEAN: the variance risk premium survives the trigger

The implied-realized gap: VXN(t) minus the forward 12-week realized annualized vol (std of the next 12 weekly log returns x sqrt(52) x 100). Medians, pre-2021:

- at compression entries: +3.71 vol points (implied exceeds what realizes), positive at 73.2% of entries;
- across all weeks: +4.55, positive at 76.7%;
- forward 12-week realized at entries: 13.96 versus 17.22 all weeks.

The strong unseen-ness claim - "the market fails to price the coming expansion, so the compression entry is cheap optionality on it" - is REFUTED at the mean level in the design sample. Even at the trigger, implied vol overprices the subsequent 12 weeks on average; the premium is thinner than unconditional (+3.71 vs +4.55) but it does not invert. Buying the expansion through options at the cross is not free money in the design data: the crowd's calm is still overpriced calm.

### 1c. EVENT STRUCTURE: the honest boundary

Whether the specific first-significant-week hazard (0.634 within 12 weeks, median lag 5, the 3.5-sigma median event) is priced cannot be read from weekly index closes: that would need the options term structure (is the VXN curve flat/cheap at compression entries?) or option prices themselves (the implied probability of a >=2.5 sigma week within 12 weeks). Out of scope for weekly data; recorded, not actioned. This is the boundary of the unseen-ness question, stated as such.

## 2. The verdict, and what it does to the theory

The compression signature is LEVEL-cheap (26th percentile, the crowd prices calm at the cross) but MEAN-priced (the variance risk premium survives the trigger). The genuinely unmeasured remainder is the event-timing structure. The consequences for the framework:

1. The OBSERVER COROLLARY is strengthened, not weakened. The three honest forms of a private forecast were information others lack, speed others lack, and a calibration record others have not measured. This layer closes the first door for the compression signature at the weekly grain: the information is public (implied vol tracks the clock monotonically) and its mean content is priced (the premium survives). What remains for the SMH observer is the third door: the hazard clock as a CALIBRATION record - scoring 0.634/median-5 rows against outcomes - which no options level prices directly, because a level is not a dated hazard.
2. "The trade no one sees" is re-scoped, on the record: unseen is the wrong word for cheap; the entry is level-cheap and mean-priced, so the edge claim cannot rest on options mispricing. It rests, if anywhere, on the event-structure question (1c) and on the forward calibration record, and only scoring can settle it.
3. No trading implication is drawn. The layer is design bookkeeping; no protocol, rule, or instrument-guard change.

## 3. Caveats, frozen

1. VXN prices options on the NDX cash index, not the NQ front-month future; basis and roll differences are absorbed as noise here, consistent with the canonical file's documented roll artefacts (stage0d crosscheck).
2. The forward-realized series uses weekly closes only; microstructure inside weeks is invisible, same limitation as every SMH table.
3. n = 41 entries; medians and shares inherit episode dependence; no test is run and none is implied by the percentile or gap numbers.
4. The VIX secondary benchmark carries 250 dropped daily nulls in Yahoo's history (coverage survives at the weekly grain; VXN is the primary benchmark and has zero nulls).
5. All numbers are unsealed pre-2021 design bookkeeping. No claim, no test, no trade; the forward log stays empty until the market's next clock entry.

## 4. Computation log (honest, per programme culture)

The first selftest run caught the author's arithmetic before it froze: the all-weeks positive-gap share was hand-derived as 1 - 0.221 = 0.779, but the direct count is 0.767 (the gap is undefined, not negative, in trailing weeks without a full 12-week forward window); the selftest caught it and the constant was corrected on the record. The stage0v fetch script also carried a print-key typo (n_nonpos vs n_nonpositive) caught on its first run; fixed, and both are kept in this log per the standing culture.

# Sovereign Agent Protocol v1 (SAP v1), SMH V2.0

Installed 2026-11-19, session 125, by the Board's directive. The programme's
Chief Scientific Officer function is the delegated session layer; the Board
is Andrea. This document records the protocol AS INSTALLED, including the
three bounded deviations from the directive's literal text (in Phase 2, 3,
and the reply format), each with the reason.

## Phase 1: Constitution and frontier

NORTH_STAR.md (north_star.md): the immutable goal and the two absolute
sentences (no edge by default; absolute burden of proof). No session may
edit it. FRONTIER.md (frontier.md): the live ranked matrix of open vectors.
self_critique.log: the attack ledger; no new vector registers without a
surviving attack entry. The append-only frontier log, front register, and
gate list remain in smh_frontier_v0.md; the programme record remains
KNOWLEDGE.md. Nothing in the existing record was renumbered or rewritten.

## Phase 2: The Swarm (parallel execution)

Each scheduled session may select up to three INDEPENDENT vectors from
FRONTIER.md, one per family where lawful: data/compute, epistemic,
infrastructure; results synthesized into ONE record entry and per-vector
log lines. Bounded deviation from the directive: three is a ceiling, not a
quota, and every vector must be lawful under NORTH_STAR's gates (the
directive's examples were illustrative; nothing sealed, nothing armed, no
re-tuning). An honest empty session outranks a forced swarm. The session
contract in the smh-frontier-session skill expresses this.

## Phase 3: The Compounding loop (singularity)

Every new script or statistical test is abstracted into a reusable
meta-tool under src/utils/ (first mandated: calculate_base_rate(asset,
window); test_estimator_stability()). Bounded deviation: meta-tools land
only on Python-enabled sessions, test-covered before use, wired into
verify_all where load-bearing; frozen scripts are never retroactively
replaced by generic calls; no meta-tool re-tests frozen estimator
definitions (stability is robustness reporting, never re-tuning). The
toolbox grows only through verified additions.

## Phase 4: The Directorate report (human interface)

 Weekly, directorate_report.md: vectors executed by the swarms; new
frontiers discovered and their attack entries; what requires the Board's
signature; the proposed re-ranking of FRONTIER.md with reasoning.
Regenerated by the Sunday cadence session (and on demand), dated sections
appended, and mirrored to the owner at the sitting.

## Phase 5: The inviolable gates

Restated in full in north_star.md. They override every phase of this
protocol, every session, every lineage, forever.

## Authority map

The Board holds: signatures on anything gated, kill lines, sealed windows,
claims, trading, and the final ranking word. The CSO layer holds: HOW the
lawful work is executed, WHICH lawful vector runs next, re-ranking
proposals, and now (standing order addendum, 2026-11-19) the autonomous
re-ranking of the live matrix with recorded reasoning. Governance proof
stays in the record, not in this file: append-only, real output, refusals
logged.

## Daily session instructions

Updated session contract lives in the smh-frontier-session skill, which
routes to: NORTH_STAR.md, frontier.md, smh_frontier_v0.md (log and gates),
standing_order_autonomy.md, decision_queue_v0.md, directorate_report.md,
and self_critique.log, in that order after the record tail.

# Stage 7 paper-trading weekly decision log (template)

Used if and only if a registered rule advances to stage 7 (rule set T does
if it passes the holdout battery; rule set B if H3 survives). One entry per
Sunday evening analysis session, written BEFORE the week resolves, never
edited afterwards. Amendments are appended in a corrections section with a
reason, never overwritten.

## Entry format

```
## Week of YYYY-MM-DD (Sunday session)

- Decision close: YYYY-MM-DD (Friday that just closed)
- Price context: close XXXX.XX, 12-week log return +/-X.XX%
- Rule signal (T): LONG / FLAT (computed from data, not opinion)
- AVFI state: momentum_up / reversal_down / ... ; sp_z: X.XX
- Wave count (context only, never an entry trigger): ...
- Planned action: [exact order: instrument, direction, size fraction]
- Pre-committed levels: entry fill window, exit condition, stop if any
- Discipline check: does this action match the registered rule? YES/NO
- If NO, why: ...

(after the week resolves, append:)
- Outcome: fill price, next decision close, rule P&L in points and %
- Deviation from plan: none / describe
```

## Paper-account conventions

- Start: 100,000 USD paper equity. Risk per position: 0.5% of paper equity
  at the stop (programme section 4); if no stop (rule set T), risk is the
  full position and size is capped at 1 NQ notional equivalent or the MNQ
  fraction that keeps notional under 30% of paper equity, whichever binds.
- Fills assumed at next weekly open +/- 0.3625 pts per side (1 tick +
  commission), logged as such.
- Every week gets an entry, including NO-TRADE weeks (flat is a decision).
- Kill criteria (pre-committed, programme section 6 stage 7): two
  consecutive months of rule breaches pauses the programme; systematic
  divergence between paper results and the backtest confidence interval
  triggers investigation, not rationalisation.

## Minimum duration

26 weekly entries before any evaluation; the 26-week report compares
realised weekly mean, vol, hit rate and drawdown against the backtest
distribution, then goes to the stage 8 go/no-go document.

# Standing Order: Autonomous Session Conduct (SIGNED 2026-11-19)

Status: SIGNED 2026-11-19 at the owner's directorate sitting, his dated word given via the batched question prompt and recorded verbatim in the sign-off section below. From that date the additions in "What the owner's dated word here ADDS" are lawful session conduct: front F6 (the generative audit layer) is ungated to ACTIVE, proposal-form re-ranking is lawful, and the weekly directorate readout is the standing cadence. The sections below are the order as presented for signature; the owner-forever gate and the two load-bearing rules are unchanged by signing. Before 2026-11-19 this document was an unsigned draft with zero status: every session that touched it before that date was lawful only under the F1-F5 stewardship baseline, which remains lawful without any order.

Written 19 Nov 2026, session 108, at the owner's direction: "make scheduled sessions advance new fronts, not repeat one ritual." This order converts scheduled sessions from task execution into task selection, without moving one millimetre of the hard gate.

## What scheduled sessions may already do without this order

The stewardship baseline, fronts F1-F5 of the frontier file, each already bounded by the record's protocols: run and verify the three standing tracks (F1), rebuild and verify the tree, audit the read-only engine status, verify record integrity (F2), keep the site lagging the record by at most one session entry (F3), keep the decision queue audited and citation-checked (F4), observe the decay items (F5). Plus the drafting front F7, whose output carries zero status by construction, and the blocked preparation front F8, which touches no data.

## What the owner's dated word here ADDS

1. The generative audit layer, frontier front F6: scheduled sessions may design and run self-audits of already-registered machinery on unsealed pre-2021 data, under the pre-declared one-knob, all-cells-reported, none-selection rule set. F6 dies per audit on any selection, rule proposal, or sealed-window touch; three dead audits close the front pending the owner's word.
2. Proposal-form goal re-ranking: a session may append to the frontier log a re-proposed ranking of fronts with recorded reasoning. Proposals never move anything by themselves; the ranking changes only at the weekly directorate.
3. The weekly directorate cadence: the weekly session appends a one-page readout: current front ranking, any re-ranking proposals and their recorded reasoning, the pending-decision queue in one line each (draft location, what signing does, what refusing does), decay status, verify-chain result. One readout, one sitting; the owner batch-speaks or defers.

## What remains the owner's forever, signed or not

Arming, ratifying, or executing any amendment; moving any kill line, frozen constant, registered default, rule set, protocol, or observation horizon; re-tuning anything post-null; anything that evaluates, describes, or touches the spent NQ 2021+ holdout or any sealed 2021+ macro or BTC window; running the original battery or the stage2b H8/H9 scripts; closing, re-opening, or amending any claim verdict; anything that trades or recommends trading; rewriting record history; touching the parallel lineage.

This is not a limitation on the autonomy. It is the thing that makes the autonomy evidence-grade. A self-directed agent with no owner gate converges on laundering its own hypotheses; the falsification record is the programme's entire value, and it stays falsifiable only while every tuning surface stays behind the owner's word. The default assumption (no edge) and the burden of proof (on every signal) remain load-bearing regardless of this order's state.

# V4h Booking Audit v0: a checkable verdict for "verify V4h's first nightly bookings"

Status: FORWARD-TRACK TOOLING, authored 12 Nov 2026, session 51, PROGRESS
block 59, on the hourly mandate ("advance the SMH research by one concrete
step"). The instrument is READ-ONLY: it never books, never writes any file,
never touches the engine's state/log/card/report, never duplicates or
accelerates the V4h nightly 23:30 Malta task, and never runs the booking
machinery. It exists because the Sunday session checklist carries "verify
V4h's first nightly bookings" and, until now, that verification was manual
eyeballing of a CSV. It is now a checkable verdict.

Engine audited: research/v4h_bot.py (the rule's ONLY booking machinery). Spec:
docs/v4h_spec.md (REGISTERED 2026-11-12, frozen). The audit's constants are
duplicated from the frozen spec ON PURPOSE, so the audit checks the engine
against the spec, not against the engine's own live state.

## 1. What it checks (every item derived from the frozen spec)

State level (holds even with zero bookings, the current state of the track):

- track_start exists and does not predate registration (2026-11-12 02:42:18
  Malta = 01:42:18 UTC); equity positive.

Per booked row (once the log exists):

- GEOMETRY: bar_end = bar_start + 4h; every bar_start sits on a globex anchor
  hour (18/22/02/06/10/14 ET, checked in ET, not UTC); every bar_end >
  track_start (the spec forbids pre-registration booking entirely).
- APPEND-ONLY IDEMPOTENCE: no duplicate bar keys, bar_start strictly
  increasing.
- RULE IDENTITIES: 0 <= pos <= 1 (cap); tgt in {0.20, 0.15} and 0.15 exactly
  when state4h is reversal_down; the DECISION pos_next_decided is 0 whenever
  the same-row mom12h <= 0 (long-only, signal exits only); the SIZING
  IDENTITY pos_next_decided = min(1, tgt/vol8h) under the long condition.
- THE 1-BAR LAG: pos_held[t] = pos_next_decided[t-1]; the first booked row
  must hold 0 (registration initializes pos_next = 0, so a nonzero
  pos_held[0] implies pre-track booking); dpos[t] = |pos_held[t] -
  pos_held[t-1]|; cost_frac[t] = dpos[t] x 0.3625 / close[t].
- THE EQUITY CHAIN: recomputed from 100,000 as eq *= 1 + pos*ret - cost_frac
  with ret from consecutive logged closes. Verifiable only between CONTIGUOUS
  booked buckets; a gap (skipped holiday bucket, first booked row, or an
  engine catch-up boundary) marks the row ret-unverifiable and the chain
  resumes from the logged equity there - by design, never a FAIL. The
  cumulative tolerance is 0.06/row plus a flat 0.02, which dominates the
  engine's rounding (close 2dp, pos 4dp, cost_frac 6dp, equity 2dp).
- STATE COHERENCE: state equity ~= last equity_after (2dp); state pos_next ~=
  last pos_next_decided; state last_booked == last bar_end (idempotence
  evidence); a stale run-report last_booked is a WARNING, not a failure.

Verdicts: CLEAN, CLEAN_NO_BOOKINGS (the track's current state: armed, not
evaluated), or FAIL with an itemized failure list.

## 2. Modes

- `python3 research/v4h_audit.py` - status: audit the bundle's data/v4h/
  files, print the JSON verdict. Zero network, zero writes.
- `python3 research/v4h_audit.py --recompute` - additionally fetch fresh
  hourly data, rebuild the 4h grid with the ENGINE'S OWN build_4h, and
  cross-check every booked row's close/state4h/vol8h_ann/mom12h against the
  fresh recomputation. Still read-only; minor close/state revisions inside
  the lookback window are possible Yahoo artefacts, so any mismatch is
  investigated before it is trusted. Intended for the Sunday session, on a
  Python-enabled machine, AFTER the nightly run has booked.
- `python3 research/v4h_audit.py selftest` - pure in-memory: a synthetic
  clean track (6 bars, decisions built BY THE RULE, positions carrying the
  1-bar lag, honest cost and equity chains) must audit CLEAN, and each of 12
  seeded violations must be CAUGHT: pre-registration track_start, duplicate
  bar keys, cap breach, tgt/state mismatch, decision-lag break, dpos break,
  cost break, equity-chain break, anchor-hour break, state-equity break,
  last_booked break, long-only break. No network, no bundle access.

## 3. Honest computation log

Authored without Python in the session sandbox (the normal reset state), so
the selftest has not been executed yet; it is to be run on the next
Python-enabled session BEFORE the first Sunday use, and if it passes the
tool may be wired into verify_all.sh as a 22nd check (that wiring is
deliberately NOT done in this session - a check that has never run does not
belong in the chain). Three design errors were caught during authoring by
re-deriving each check against the engine's actual booking loop:

1. THE LONG-ONLY CHECK WAS ON THE WRONG SERIES: the first draft flagged
   pos_held > 0 on a mom12h <= 0 row. That is not a violation - with the
   1-bar lag a position decided at the previous close legitimately survives
   a same-row mom flip. The check was moved to the DECISION
   (pos_next_decided vs same-row mom12h), where the rule actually binds.
2. THE ANCHOR-HOUR CHECK WAS IN THE WRONG TIMEZONE: the first draft
   extracted bar_start hours after converting to UTC, which would have
   compared ET anchor hours against UTC hours and false-flagged every bar.
   The check now converts to America/New_York first.
3. THE FIRST-ROW dpos CHECK WAS UNRECOVERABLE: the first draft checked
   dpos[0] against the CURRENT state pos_prev, but that field advances on
   every run and no longer holds its booking-time value. The spec's
   registration invariant replaces it: pos_next initializes to 0, so the
   first booked row must hold exactly 0, and dpos[0] = pos_held[0] follows.

A fourth softening: the engine rounds its logged values, so the equity
chain is recomputed from ROUNDED inputs and carries a documented cumulative
tolerance instead of exact equality.

## 4. What this instrument is NOT

No performance statistic lives in it: equity, positions and costs are
bookkeeping identities, and a CLEAN verdict certifies only that the
bookkeeping is faithful to the frozen spec. It is not strategy evaluation,
produces no p-value, compares nothing against any benchmark, and says
nothing about whether V4h has an edge - the V4h evidence path is the live
forward paper track and nothing else. The 2021+ NQ holdout is spent and
irrelevant here by construction. Paper only.

## 5. Intended use (the Sunday 2026-11-15 session)

After the nightly run has booked and the stage 7 sequence is done: run
`python3 research/v4h_audit.py --recompute`, read the verdict, and record
it in the Sunday log. CLEAN = the first bookings are verified faithful to
the frozen spec; FAIL = itemized, investigated, and recorded as found. The
audit never modifies anything, so a FAIL costs nothing but knowledge.

## 6. Session-56 addendum (12 Nov 2026): TS-mirror validation executed; a REAL
instrument defect found and fixed before the first run

No Python in the session-56 sandbox (the normal reset state), so the audit was
validated by the programme's standing two-layer convention: a faithful
TypeScript mirror (research/v4h_audit_ts_mirror.ts, report
research/v4h_audit_mirror_report.json) of audit_track() + the full selftest,
executed in-session.

MIRROR RESULT: 14/14 checks pass (clean track CLEAN; all 12 seeded violations
caught; zero-booking state CLEAN_NO_BOOKINGS), and the REAL armed track state
(data/v4h: 0 bars booked, log absent) audits CLEAN_NO_BOOKINGS exactly as the
instrument's armed-state path requires.

THE DEFECT THE MIRROR CAUGHT (a fourth authoring error, found only by
executing): the equity chain was checked against the LAST row's logged
equity_after only. Seeded violation m8 (an INTERIOR row's equity_after
corrupted by -50) audited CLEAN: the recomputed chain never reads interior
equity_after values, so an interior bookkeeping corruption in the live track
would have passed the audit, and the instrument's own Python selftest would
have FAILED on its m8 assertion at first run. Fixed BEFORE any real use:
audit_track() now recomputes the chain and compares against EVERY row's
logged equity_after (per-row tolerance CHAIN_FLAT_TOL + CHAIN_ROW_TOL x
rows-since-resync, resyncing at gaps as before, first mismatch reported).
The mirror-side re-validation after the fix: 14/14 pass, m8 caught at row 4.

Mirror-side fixture bugs fixed (the audit logic was never wrong): isoET()
added the EST offset instead of subtracting it, shifting every synthetic
timestamp +10h and false-failing the anchor-hour check on the clean track.

UNCHANGED: the pandas selftest still runs FIRST on the next Python-enabled
reset, BEFORE --recompute use and BEFORE any verify_all wiring (the
session-51 rule holds: a check that has never run does not belong in the
chain). Expected outcome after the fix: SELFTEST PASS. Nothing else in the
instrument, the engine, the spec, or the live track was touched.

# Rule set V4h: the 4-hour live paper-bot edition (experimental)

Status: REGISTERED 2026-11-12 for LIVE 4-HOUR FORWARD PAPER TRACKING as a
separate experimental track, on Andrea's explicit instruction ("make a 4h
tf live paper trading nq bot with v4 working live") and his recorded
sign-off on the design (V4h experimental row + live weekly T/U3/V context
card; daily run 23:30 Malta). It is NOT the weekly rule set V. The weekly
programme (stage 7 Sunday log, T tracked, U3/V parallel) is unaffected by
this track and continues unchanged.

## Why this is a new rule set and not a port

The weekly v4 indicator hardcodes the weekly annualization
sqrt(52) in its vol8. Pasted onto a 4h chart it would silently disable
volatility targeting (vol8 would read as near-zero annualized, the vol
factor would sit at its 1.0 cap, and the rule would degenerate to an
unsized 48h momentum). A 4h edition therefore requires a re-derived
annualization, which makes it a NEW rule with its own frozen spec.

## Evidence status, stated plainly

  * NO p-value exists for V4h and none may ever be computed. No
    backtest will be run: the 2021+ holdout is SPENT, V4h has no
    unsealed/holdout distinction at all, and its bars before registration
    (2026-11-12) are indicator warmup, never booked. The engine books
    only bars completed AFTER the registration timestamp; it refuses to
    book history.
  * The ONLY evaluation path is this live forward paper track, judged
    the same way as everything else in the programme: by accumulated
    forward evidence, pre-committed standards and honest bookkeeping,
    never by a fitted backtest.
  * CHALLENGE ASSUMPTIONS, RECORDED AT REGISTRATION: the programme's
    whole record says timing variants died (27 of 27 AVFI entry variants;
    every weekly-timing claim null; the generic weekly trend is the only
    replicated edge). Nothing in the record supports 4h timing. V4h is
    being run to MEASURE that honestly, not because the record favours
    it. The 48h momentum flip rate plus per-bar rebalancing churn is
    expected to be the main cost; if the track shows that, the honest
    verdict is "null at 4h", the same as the weekly family's nulls.

## The rule (frozen at registration; never re-tuned)

Bars: CME globex-anchored 4h buckets of hourly NQ=F data, anchored at
the 18:00 ET session open: buckets [18-22), [22-02), [02-06), [06-10),
[10-14), [14-17) ET (the last bucket of each globex day is the 3-hour
14:00-17:00 segment; 17:00-18:00 is the maintenance break). 30 buckets
per week. A bucket is COMPLETED when its end time has passed and it
holds data. Buckets with no data (holidays) are skipped.

Signal (structure of rule set V, re-derived for 4h bars):

  mom12h = log(close_t / close_{t-12}) at each completed 4h close
           (a 48-hour trend, exactly what the v4 Pine would compute
           on a 4h chart).
  Long when mom12h > 0, flat otherwise. Signal exits only.

Sizing (the V mechanism, correctly annualized):

  vol8h = 8-bar sample stdev (ddof=1) of 4h log returns,
          annualized x sqrt(1560) (30 bars/week x 52).
  tgt   = 0.15 if the 4h AVFI state at the decision close is
          reversal_down, else 0.20 (unchanged AVFI core constants:
          hlVol 30, hlFlow 65, k 1.0, deadZone 0.2, gamma 2.5,
          smooth 3, warmup 260, emaLen 105, normWin 500, evaluated on
          the 4h bar grid).
  size  = min(1.0, tgt / vol8h) when long, 0 when flat. No leverage,
          no short side.

Frozen constants: momLen 12 bars, volWin 8 bars, barsPerWeek 30,
volTgt 0.20, defTgt 0.15, cap 1.0, cost 0.3625 pts/side per unit
turnover.

## Paper booking model (identical to the programme's bookkeeping
convention, stage 0h)

  * Decision at each completed 4h close; the size decided at close t
    is the position held during bar t+1 (1-bar lag, close-to-close
    marking - the same convention the weekly bookkeeping uses).
  * Cost 0.3625 pts/side charged on the absolute change in position
    (|dsize|), so per-bar rebalancing churn is charged honestly.
  * Paper equity starts at 100,000.00 USD at registration and
    compounds multiplicatively: equity *= (1 + size*ret - cost).
  * Booking is idempotent: the engine stores its last-booked bar and
    only appends bars completed after it. Re-running changes nothing.
  * Track start: 2026-11-12 (registration). No earlier bar is ever
    booked, by any run, under any circumstance.

## Engine and operation

  research/v4h_bot.py, stdlib+pandas, self-contained in the bundle
  (no working-tree dependency). Data: Yahoo v8 chart endpoint, NQ=F
  hourly (730 days, enough for the 4h AVFI warmup: 260+105+500 =
    865 4h bars < ~2,900 available) plus NQ=F dailies for the weekly
  context card. Every run:
    1. fetch fresh hourly + daily data,
    2. build the 4h bars, compute the 4h AVFI and the V4h signal,
    3. book every completed bar since the last run (append-only
       CSV log + state JSON in data/v4h/),
    4. compute the weekly context card (observation only: T/U3/V
       weekly states from fresh dailies; NO weekly booking - the
       weekly stage 7 Sunday session remains the sole booking
       machinery for the weekly rules),
    5. write v4h_card.json and print the run summary.
  Schedule: daily 23:30 Europe/Malta (after the 17:00 ET close,
  ~30 min for data settlement), via the workspace scheduled task.
  The scheduler cannot fire sub-daily; the engine compensates by
  booking all completed bars each run, which reproduces true
  4h-cadence decisions (decisions happen at bar closes, so a daily
  catch-up books exactly what a 4h bot would have booked).

## Deliberately rejected for V4h

  * Intradabar fills, stops, or take-profits (bar-close decisions
    only, the programme's cadence discipline).
  * The weekly stage 7 paper account's 30% notional cap (V4h uses the
    clean size-times-equity bookkeeping model of stage 0h, so the
    rule's risk meaning is preserved).
  * |sp_z| filters, TB/PGT/v3.4 layers as inputs, short side,
    leverage, parameter adaptation of any kind.
  * Any backtest or historical booking.

Registered by: programme session 22, 2026-11-12, on Andrea's
instruction and sign-off.
